Result: 在40,000条验证序列上，PL0与PL2的池化排名几乎相同，但位置Recall@1分别为13.5%和98.8%；在3,930条LJSpeech验证集上，冻结PL2加MatchaTTS读出的WER为21.54%，PL0为99.22%，端到端MatchaTTS为10.93%。

Conclusion: 仅靠几何规律性不能保证可恢复的序列内容或有效的下游访问，文本设置下需显式监督与读出对齐。

Abstract: We study sequential content sufficiency by investigating whether a representation retains the ordered target information available in its input. An information-theoretic decomposition separates input ambiguity, representation loss, and readout mismatch. We construct recoverable views where perfect agreement and joint isotropic Gaussianity coexist with zero target information, and establish limits imposed by deterministic canonical anchors. Token log-loss provides a one-sided information-loss bound; a fixed-penalty ridge analysis shows why rank alone cannot determine prediction risk. These results motivate CANOPE, a nonautoregressive framework with ordered latent canvases, canonical-token supervision, and geometric regularization. On 40,000 validation sequences, latent-agreement (PL0) and token-grounded (PL2) have nearly identical pooled ranks but reach 13.5% and 98.8% positional Recall@1, respectively, under strong natural corruption when the correct target length is provided. On 3,930 LJSpeech validation utterances, frozen PL2 with a trained MatchaTTS readout yields 21.54% word error rate (WER) on corrupted text, versus 99.22% for frozen PL0, while end-to-end MatchaTTS reaches 10.93%. These results show that geometric regularity alone does not guarantee recoverable sequential content or effective downstream access in the text settings studied here.

</details>


### [133] [Complementary Feature Domains: Information Preservation Does Not Imply Predictive-Contribution Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07565)
*Timothy Oladunni,Farouk Ganiyu-Adewumi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 互补特征域（CFD）理论将预测价值刻画为表示及其实现族共同诱导的、由上下文索引的贡献系统，并证明Shannon信息保持不蕴含该贡献系统的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将信息保持等同于预测价值保持，但表示的可逆变换可能在不改变目标信息的情况下改变受限决策族下的预测贡献，因此需要形式化信息保持与实现相关贡献之间的分离。

Method: 提出CFD贡献缺陷度量受控重编码下上下文贡献的变化；针对有界Lipschitz效用，证明每个联盟效用偏移受重编码前后可达动作集的行为距离界定，进而每个上下文贡献缺陷受相应联盟不兼容性之和界定；并通过受控ECG实验验证。

Result: 理论表明精确行为闭包导致不变性，而逐渐精确的补偿导致恢复；ECG实验中非线性双射重编码保持冻结时频表示中的信息，但改变固定仿射学习器的准确率，应用精确逆变换可恢复所有测试联盟准确率。

Conclusion: 该结果将信息保持与实现相关贡献分离，并为多表示预测提供了定量的转变规律。

Abstract: Complementary Feature Domains (CFD) theory characterizes predictive value as a context-indexed contribution system induced jointly by representations and their realization family. We show that Shannon-information preservation does not imply preservation of this contribution system: an invertible representation transformation can leave target information unchanged while altering predictive contribution under a restricted decision family. We formalize the resulting transition through a CFD contribution defect that measures how contextual contributions change under controlled recoding. For bounded Lipschitz utility, we show that each coalition utility shift is bounded by the behavioral distance between the attainable action sets before and after recoding; consequently, every contextual contribution defect is bounded by the sum of the corresponding coalition incompatibilities. Exact behavioral closure yields invariance, while increasingly accurate compensation yields restoration. A controlled ECG experiment illustrates the mechanism: a nonlinear bijective recoding preserves the information in a frozen time-frequency representation but changes accuracy under a fixed affine learner; applying the exact inverse restores all tested coalition accuracies. The result separates information preservation from realization-dependent contribution and provides a quantitative transition law for multi-representation prediction.

</details>


### [134] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Atmospheric Rivers in GraphCast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07583)
*Madelyn Mathai,Timothy B. Higgins,Kevin M. Grise,Chirag Agarwal,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在GraphCast中提取学习到的内部概念，发现模型内部稳定计算了大气河强度（IVT），尽管IVT既非输入也非目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI气象模型虽能媲美业务预报，但其内部如何表征大气尚不明确；特征归因只能揭示哪些输入重要，无法说明模型内部计算什么以及如何组合信息。

Method: 在GraphCast上训练标准稀疏自编码器和Matryoshka稀疏自编码器，以大气河为研究对象，提取并排序模型内部概念，并通过干预实验验证因果关系。

Result: 两种SAE均显示GraphCast将大气河强度（以积分水汽输送IVT衡量）计算为稳定的内部变量；Matryoshka SAE按重要性排序概念并揭示其关系，大气河概念跨网络深度持续存在，干预实验证实其因果作用。

Conclusion: 该方法能够发现模型内部变量并确定其实际依赖的变量，为评估这些变量在气候变暖下现象变化时是否仍具意义提供了前提。

Abstract: While AI weather models now rival operational forecasts, how they represent the atmosphere internally remains an open question: feature attribution reveals which input patterns matter, not what the model computes or how it combines information internally. We train sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on GraphCast to uncover its learned concepts, using atmospheric rivers as our phenomenon of focus. Both standard and Matryoshka SAEs show GraphCast computes atmospheric river intensity, measured by integrated vapor transport (IVT), as a stable internal variable, despite IVT being neither an input nor a target. In contrast to the unstructured concept retrieval of the standard SAE, the Matryoshka SAE orders concepts by importance and exposes their relations. Atmospheric river concepts persist across depth and direct interventions confirm causality. This method offers a way to find internal variables and determine which of them the model actually relies on, which is a prerequisite for asking whether those variables remain meaningful as the phenomenon changes under a warming climate.

</details>


### [135] [Hub for Outliers, Spokes for Inliers: Uniform Latent Space Construction for Dual-Mismatched Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07610)
*Li Yuan,Yaxin Hou,Jiawei Tang,Yongbiao Gao,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种枢纽-辐条潜在几何结构，解决半监督学习中类别分布与标签空间双重不匹配问题，提升特征判别性与伪标签质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督学习通常假设有标签与无标签数据共享相同类别分布和标签空间，但实际中无标签数据可能不平衡且包含未知类样本，导致多数类主导潜在空间、未知类样本被过度自信误分类，降低特征判别性和伪标签质量。

Method: 设计枢纽-辐条潜在几何：已知类均匀分布在中心枢纽周围，每类围绕其原型形成紧凑簇；枢纽为高不确定性未知类样本提供低证据区域锚点。结合基于证据的分类器，通过结构化特征组织缓解多数类主导，引导高不确定性未知类样本朝向枢纽，增强特征判别性和不确定性分离。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法优于现有最先进方法，在不同设置下最大提升3.25%。

Conclusion: 所提枢纽-辐条潜在几何与证据分类器结合，有效应对类别分布与标签空间双重不匹配，提升半监督学习性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning typically assumes that labeled and unlabeled data share an identical class distribution and label space. However, this setting is often violated: unlabeled data may be imbalanced and contain unknown class samples, causing mismatches in both class distribution and label space. Such dual mismatch leads to majority classes dominating the latent space and unknown class samples being overconfidently misclassified, degrading feature discriminability and pseudo-label quality. To address this, we propose a hub-spoke latent geometry, where known classes are uniformly distributed around a central hub and each class forms compact clusters around its prototype, while the hub provides an anchor for a low-evidence region specifically designed for high-uncertainty unknown class samples. Integrated with an evidence-based classifier, this geometry ultimately enhances feature discriminability and uncertainty separation by mitigating majority-class domination through structured feature organization and guiding high-uncertainty unknown class samples toward the hub. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with a maximum improvement of 3.25% across various settings.

</details>


### [136] [Learning from Revision Consequences: Hindsight Meta-Experience Distillation for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07979)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出HMED机制，通过从同一恢复的发现状态重执行新旧Meta-Skill来构建Meta-Experience，以消除初始状态与修订效果的混淆，提升自我改进智能体的技能发现性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从原始技能搜索轨迹和分支结果改进Meta-Skill，但分支性能混合了初始发现状态与Meta-Skill修订的影响，难以刻画修订实际改变了什么，导致更新偏向受益于有利状态的修订，而非真正改进过程的修订。

Method: HMED（Hindsight Meta-Experience Distillation）回溯修订所源自的已完成事件，在相同恢复的发现状态下重新执行 incumbent 与 revised Meta-Skill，在共享条件下观察修订带来的变化，并将每次比较蒸馏为结构化的Meta-Experience记录，供未来更新复用，使未被保留的修订也能提供学习信号。

Result: 在三个交互式智能体基准上，使用开源和闭源模型，HMED在技能发现性能上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: HMED将Meta-Skill学习从依赖分支结果转向关注改进过程本身的变化后果，有效提升了自我改进智能体的技能发现能力。

Abstract: As agents continuously improve by generating and revising Skills, the process that discovers and refines those Skills becomes a learnable object in its own right. Task-Skills directly act on task execution, whereas Meta-Skills govern how agents discover and improve future Skills; their value therefore emerges through the subsequent search processes they induce. Existing approaches improve Meta-Skills from observed raw Skill-search trajectories and branch outcomes. However, branch performance entangles the effects of the initial discovery state and the Meta-Skill revision that generated the search process, making it difficult to characterize what a particular revision actually changed, and pushing updates toward revisions that benefit from favorable states rather than those that improve the process. We introduce HMED (Hindsight Meta-Experience Distillation), a mechanism for constructing Meta-Experience for self-improving agents. HMED revisits the completed event from which a revision originates and re-executes the incumbent and revised Meta-Skills from the same restored discovery state, so that the changes associated with the revision can be observed under a shared condition. Each comparison is distilled into a Meta-Experience, a structured record that can be reused by future updates, so that even revisions that are not ultimately retained still contribute a learning signal. Across three interactive agent benchmarks and both open-source and closed-source models, HMED consistently improves Skill discovery performance over strong baselines, shifting Meta-Skill learning beyond branch outcomes toward the consequences of changing the improvement process.

</details>


### [137] [Same Feedback, Different Answer: Measuring Run-to-Run Instability in Frontier-Model Customer Feedback Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08036)
*Viraj Bagal,Raviraja Ganta,Prabhath Chellingi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出重复运行评估框架，发现分类学锚定的智能体（TGA）在客户反馈任务中显著降低主题变动和数量分歧，提升输出的可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体自动化知识工作时，需要保证在证据不变时多次运行的类别、优先级和计数一致，但现有方法缺乏对重复运行稳定性的评估。

Method: 设计重复运行评估框架，对齐语义等价类别，定义主题变动（类别集合的归一化变化）和数量分歧（持续类别计数的变化）两个指标。在三个客户反馈任务上，使用八个前沿模型、语料规模100-5000条、多种提示，比较原始生成、无分类学层次分解和基于分类学的智能体（TGA）三种执行设计。

Result: 在Claude Opus 4.8和1000条语料下，TGA比原始生成和层次分解减少86-88%的主题变动，匹配主题的数量分歧为零。TGA比所有原始模型更稳定，在各语料规模下均保持优势，且匹配严格或宽松时仍稳定。

Conclusion: 分类学锚定能生成更一致、可重复的输出，适用于客户反馈及其他非结构化语料（如金融报告、法律文件、事件记录、科学文献）的重复综合任务。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being programmed to automate knowledge work over large collections of unstructured data. Such automation requires repeatability: when the underlying evidence is unchanged, the agent's categories, priorities, and counts should not shift materially between runs, even if each individual answer appears plausible. We introduce a repeat-run evaluation framework that aligns semantically equivalent categories and focuses on two operating metrics: theme churn, the normalized change in the returned category set, and volume disagreement, the change in counts for categories that persist. We evaluate three recurring customer-feedback tasks across eight frontier models, corpus sizes from 100 to 5,000 records, multiple prompts, and three execution designs: raw generation, taxonomy-free hierarchical decomposition, and a taxonomy-grounded agent (TGA) using persistent themes, subthemes, and record-level predictions. With Claude Opus 4.8 and the 1,000-record corpus fixed, TGA reduces theme churn by 86--88% relative to both raw generation and hierarchical decomposition, while matched-theme volumes have zero disagreement. The taxonomy-grounded agent is more stable than every raw model in the screen, remains more stable at each corpus size, and keeps this advantage when theme matching is made stricter or looser. Although evaluated on customer feedback, the framework targets repeated synthesis of unstructured corpora more broadly, including financial reports, legal documents, incident records, and scientific literature. Overall, these results show that taxonomy grounding produces more consistent and repeatable outputs for recurring knowledge work.

</details>


### [138] [MS-ECG-FM: Towards a More Universal Electrocardiogram Foundation Model for Health Monitoring using Multi-source Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07662)
*Robert A. Lewis,I-Min Chiu,Kyle Verrier,Karthik Jayaraman Raghuram,Francoise Marvel,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Guillermo Sapiro,Andrew C. Miller,Joseph Futoma*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出多源临床报告对比对齐的心电基础模型MS-ECG-FM，在多项心电检测任务上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电基础模型仅依赖心电解读报告作为监督，忽略了波形中更广泛的诊断信号，限制了表征学习。

Method: 通过对比对齐将心电信号与多种临床报告（心电、超声心动、放射、出院报告）进行多源对齐训练。

Result: 在扩展的心电检测基准上，MS-ECG-FM在可检测的各类病症上全面优于现有方法，包括减少导联配置。

Conclusion: 不同报告分别提升不同诊断领域的表征，多源对齐能整合互补信息，在临床多样任务上产生稳定强表征。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) records the electrical activity of the heart, aiding diagnosis by detecting abnormalities in cardiac function. ECG foundation models have demonstrated promising results, but are limited by a reliance on ECG interpretation reports as their sole supervision. Because interpretation reports only capture the subset of waveform information routinely recognized by clinicians, this constrains representation learning to overlook the broader diagnostic signals present in ECG. We introduce a new ECG foundation model --- MS-ECG-FM --- that is trained through contrastive alignment to multiple distinct clinical note types, including ECG, echocardiography, radiology, and discharge reports. We evaluate MS-ECG-FM on an extended set of ECG detection benchmarks, showing that it comprehensively outperforms existing methods on the full span of conditions that ECG can detect, including in reduced-lead configurations. Different reports improve representations for different diagnostic domains, while multi-source alignment captures their complementary information and produces consistently strong representations across clinically diverse tasks.

</details>


### [139] [Extending Pathwise Gradients to Discrete Random Variables via Finite-Order Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07786)
*Donghan He,Luhuan Wu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通用框架，为泊松等离散随机变量构造有限阶精确路径梯度估计器，该估计器是无偏解中权重方差最小的最小范数解，保持硬前向采样且无需温度调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径梯度对连续变量具有无偏、低方差、单样本可用等优势，但对离散变量通常无法精确成立，现有分类变量或增广表示方法会引入额外方差和计算开销。

Method: 构造有限阶精确路径梯度估计器，选择在所有对次数不超过某阶的多项式无偏的解中具有最小范数的解，并推导非渐近偏差界以分析对指定类之外函数的近似偏差。

Result: 该估计器唯一且最小化权重方差，保留硬前向采样，无需温度调节，代码实现简单；在实验中的低阶方法在线性、非线性和层次潜变量模型上匹配或优于调优基线，且在所有运行时基准中速度最快。

Conclusion: 为离散变量提供了一种通用、精确、低方差且高效的路径梯度估计框架，克服了现有近似方法的方差与计算缺陷，在多种模型和效率上表现优异。

Abstract: Pathwise gradients are preferred for continuous random variables because they are unbiased, low variance, and work with a single sample. For discrete variables, however, the pathwise identity cannot generally be exact for every differentiable function. We propose a general framework to construct finite-order exact pathwise gradient estimators for a range of common discrete variables such as Poisson. The estimator is the least-norm solution among all solutions that are unbiased for polynomials of degree at most. The resulting estimators preserve the hard forward sample, require no temperature tuning, and can be implemented in a few lines of codes. Against other admissible solutions, our estimator is unique and minimizes weight variance; in contrast, prior works use categorical variables or augmented representations to approximate non-categorical variables that induces excess variance and computations. To understand approximation bias for functions beyond the prescribed class, we also derive a non-asymptotic bias bound. In experiments our low order methods match or improve tuned baselines across linear, nonlinear and hierarchical latent-variable models, while out-speeding competitors in every runtime benchmark.

</details>


### [140] [Which alloy composition,what process parameters? Inferring the recipe from optimized metallic microstructure and texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08165)
*Mahish K. Guru,Jan Bohlen,Louam Lemjid,Marius Tacke,Roland Aydin,Noomane Ben Khalifa*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究探讨能否从镁合金的微观组织和织构反推其合金成分与加工参数，比较了传统统计描述符、预训练图像嵌入和图神经网络三种表征，并结合分类预测头完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合金开发正向是从成分与工艺到组织再到性能，而反向从优化组织回溯到成分与工艺仍依赖专家知识，作者希望验证这一反向步骤是否可学习。

Method: 使用包含14种镁合金、107个挤压条件的内部数据集，每个条件有光学显微图和X射线织构测量；比较传统晶粒与织构统计、预训练图像编码器的视觉嵌入、晶粒网络图神经网络三种描述符；分别搭配预测头完成任务A（由工艺预测合金成分）和任务B（由成分预测工艺参数），采用5折交叉验证。

Result: 传统描述符在留出条件中识别正确合金的准确率为65%，而始终猜最常见合金仅为17%，学习嵌入低于30%；工艺参数可恢复但噪声更大，与仅用成分相比，微观组织使温度误差约减半。

Conclusion: 反向从组织到成分和工艺的映射可以部分学习，传统统计描述符在成分识别上表现最佳；由于合金和压机设置选项有限，两个任务答案离散，尊重顺序的离散选项预测头优于自由值预测头。

Abstract: The mechanical properties of a metallic alloy are set by its microstructure and texture: the size and shape of its grains and the orientation of their crystals. That structure is in turn set by a recipe, the alloy composition together with the processing parameters. Alloy development runs this chain forwards, tuning the structure until a target property is met. Running it backwards, from an optimized structure to the recipe that would produce it, still relies on expert knowledge. We ask whether this backwards step can be learned. On an in-house dataset of 107 magnesium alloy extrusion conditions across 14 alloys, each with optical micrographs and an X-ray texture measurement, we compare three descriptors of microstructure and texture: conventional grain and texture statistics, a vision embedding from a pretrained image encoder, and a graph neural network on the grain network. Each is paired with prediction heads for two tasks: the alloy composition given the process (Task A), and the process parameters given the composition (Task B). Under 5-fold cross-validation, the conventional descriptors identify the correct alloy for 65% of held-out conditions, against 17% for always guessing the most common alloy, while the learned embeddings stay below 30%. The process parameters are recoverable but noisier: compared with using the composition alone, the microstructure roughly halves the temperature error. Because only a few alloys were cast and only a few press settings were used, both answers are discrete, and heads that pick from these known options, while respecting their order, worked better than heads that predict a free value.

</details>


### [141] [LFHE: Local-First Heuristic Evolution for Bounded Local Topology Search in Decentralized Learning with Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08176)
*Yin-Kuan Liang,Yan Gao,Yang Long*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究有界局部拓扑搜索下的去中心化学习重连问题，提出仅利用自我邻域和友友信息的 Local-First Heuristic Evolution (LFHE) 框架，并在四个基准上验证其竞争性能与局部性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习在非独立同分布数据下对通信拓扑高度敏感；自适应对等选择可利用局部模型信息，但更广的对等发现需要越来越大的控制状态，而直接谱优化通常依赖全图信息。因此需要研究介于两者之间的有界局部拓扑搜索。

Method: 提出表示驱动的重连框架 LFHE，其候选发现和评分仅使用自我邻域和友友（FoF）信息。结构分数通过图 Dirichlet 能量获得精确解释：其跨客户端之和等于两倍的表示 Dirichlet 能量，在标准线性共识动力学下控制表示分歧的瞬时耗散。LFHE 将该状态相关结构信号与早期探索和度控制结合，同时将代数连通性作为离线图诊断。在有界稀疏度下，FoF 候选状态保持局部性，不扩展为全群体对等跟踪。

Result: 在四个图像、语音和文本基准上，LFHE 取得有竞争力的去中心化学习性能。匹配协议对照表明结构项是主要的经验拓扑选择信号；与更广对等发现的比较揭示了预测性能与发现状态局部性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持在对等选择与全局知情拓扑优化之间进行状态感知的有界局部拓扑搜索。

Abstract: Decentralized learning is highly sensitive to communication topology under non-IID data. Adaptive peer-selection methods can exploit local model information, but broader peer discovery may require increasingly large control state, whereas direct spectral optimization typically relies on graph-wide information. We study the intermediate setting of bounded local topology search and propose Local-First Heuristic Evolution (LFHE), a representation-driven rewiring framework whose candidate discovery and scoring use only ego-neighborhood and friend-of-a-friend (FoF) information. The structural score admits an exact interpretation through graph Dirichlet energy: its sum across clients equals twice the representation Dirichlet energy, which under standard linear consensus dynamics governs the instantaneous dissipation of representation disagreement. LFHE combines this state-dependent structural signal with early exploration and degree control, while algebraic connectivity remains an offline graph diagnostic. Under bounded sparse degree, its FoF candidate state remains local rather than expanding toward population-wide peer tracking. Across four image, speech, and text benchmarks, LFHE achieves competitive decentralized learning performance. Matched-protocol controls identify the structural term as the principal empirical topology-selection signal, while comparison with broader peer discovery exposes a trade-off between predictive performance and discovery-state locality. Together, these results motivate state-aware bounded local topology search between pairwise peer selection and globally informed topology optimization.

</details>


### [142] [The Geometry of Empowerment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07796)
*Catherine Ji,Vivek Myers,Sergey Levine,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文将赋权最大化与技能学习方法相联系，为解释和分析赋权提供了新的几何视角，并回答了赋权与结构中心性之间长期存在的开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋权被概念化为一种信息论量，用于衡量智能体主动控制环境的能力，但其与提供广泛未来结果访问的结构中心状态之间的联系一直未明。

Method: 通过将赋权最大化与技能学习方法相结合，构建新的几何框架来分析和解释赋权，并对比信息几何与奖励几何的差异。

Result: 分析回答了赋权与结构中心性之间长期存在的开放性问题，并揭示了信息几何与奖励几何之间的区别。

Conclusion: 研究为构建可扩展的赋权最大化方法提供了重要的理论启示，相关网站和代码已公开。

Abstract: Empowerment captures the capacity for an agent to actively control its environment. While conceptually appealing as an information-theoretic quantity, the connection between empowerment and structurally central states that provide broad access to future outcomes has remained an open question. In this work, we link empowerment maximization and skill-learning methods to provide new geometries for interpreting and analyzing empowerment. Our analyses answer longstanding open questions on the connections between empowerment and structural centrality. Our analyses also reveal distinctions between information and reward geometries, highlighting important theoretical implications to build scalable empowerment-maximization methods. Website and code can be found at https://empowerment-geometry.github.io/.

</details>


### [143] [Sensor-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08244)
*Yuekai Xu,Zitao Shuai,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Sensor-Language-Action（SLA）统一建模框架，并构建大规模基准与OpenSLA模型，实现感知、语言与动作的联合预测与解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器模型多止于感知（识别状态或预测结果），动作建模通常独立进行且依赖任务特定的封闭标签空间，缺乏统一语义接口。

Method: 引入SLA建模，以自然语言作为感知与行动的语义接口，统一多模态传感器观测、语言和异构动作；构建覆盖超116,000个体、79种传感器模态、60个动作组的大规模基准及多维度描述生成流水线；提出统一模型OpenSLA，支持分层动作预测、状态理解与动作解释。

Result: 在临床预测、手术室和代谢健康等真实任务上，OpenSLA性能优于当前最优方法，并展现出语言引导的证据 grounding 与对未见动作和人群的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SLA框架有效连接传感、语言与行动，OpenSLA验证了统一建模在真实场景中的优越性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Sensors are useful not only for understanding the world but also for deciding what to do next. Existing sensor models however largely stop at perception: they recognize states or predict outcomes, leaving actions modeled separately through task-specific and often closed label spaces. We introduce Sensor-Language-Action (SLA) modeling, a framework that connects multimodal sensor observations, natural language, and actions within a unified model. SLA uses language as a semantic interface between sensing and acting, allowing heterogeneous actions to be represented, predicted, and explained while remaining grounded in the underlying sensor evidence. We build a large-scale SLA benchmark consisting of datasets that span more than 116,000 individuals, 79 sensor modalities, and 60 action groups, together with a multi-faceted captioning pipeline that aligns user context, sensor dynamics, and action evidence. Building on this framework, we present OpenSLA, a unified SLA model for hierarchical action prediction, state understanding, and action explanation. Extensive experiments on real-world tasks in clinical prediction, operating rooms, and metabolic health verify its superior performance over the state-of-the-art. OpenSLA also demonstrates intriguing capabilities including language-guided evidence grounding and zero-shot generalization to unseen actions and cohorts.

</details>


### [144] [CoDe-LoRA: Mitigating the Orthogonality Dilemma in Continual Learning of LLMs via Knowledge Consolidation and Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08312)
*Maoqi Liu,Quan Fang,Yufei He*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CoDe-LoRA，一种无回放持续学习方法，通过自适应零空间投影与语义路由解决正交投影方法中的“正交性困境”，在四个骨干模型和三个基准上取得最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于低秩适应与正交投影的方法（如O-LoRA）虽能缓解灾难性遗忘，但严格的几何约束导致“正交性困境”：参数隔离过于刚性，阻碍了语义相关任务间共享表示的迁移与积累。

Method: CoDe-LoRA将学习过程解耦为“巩固通用知识”和“解耦任务特定知识”两部分，利用自适应零空间投影机制与语义路由，在知识积累与任务特定适应之间取得平衡。

Result: 在四个骨干模型和三个持续学习基准上的实验表明，CoDe-LoRA取得了最佳的平均准确率。

Conclusion: CoDe-LoRA有效缓解了正交投影方法的正交性困境，实现了持续学习中的知识迁移与任务适应平衡，验证了其方法的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for Large Language Models (LLMs) to sequentially adapt to evolving tasks. To mitigate catastrophic forgetting, recent advances implement low-rank adaptation with orthogonal projections (e.g., O-LoRA) to isolate task parameters. However, we reveal that such strict geometric constraints trigger an "Orthogonality Dilemma": rigid parameter isolation impedes the transfer and accumulation of shared representations across semantically related tasks. In this work, we propose a new replay-free method, called Consolidation and Decoupling LoRA (CoDe-LoRA), for CL of LLMs. CoDe-LoRA disentangles the learning process into Consolidating Universal Knowledge and Decoupling Task-Specific Knowledge. To achieve this, CoDe-LoRA leverages an adaptive null space projection mechanism and semantic routing to balance knowledge accumulation with task-specific adaptation. Experimental results across four backbones and three CL benchmarks show that CoDe-LoRA achieves the best average accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/Estrellajer/CoDe-LoRA.

</details>


### [145] [An AI-Assisted Formalization of the Poincaré Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08329)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Axel Delaval,Leheng Chen,Jinxuan Chen,Jie Xu,Yuxuan Liao,Jiedong Jiang,Chunlei Liu,Bin Dong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文介绍了借助AI辅助在Lean 4中形式化庞加莱猜想的工作，重点在于组织流程与人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何分析证明缺乏可复用的形式化基础设施，需要有效组织大规模形式化工作。

Method: 结合数学家准备的证明蓝图与显式里程碑声明，支持并行智能体工作，并让数学家定位阻塞点提供指导。

Result: 识别出该工作流背后的人工干预与组织选择，并形成可复用基础设施的起点。

Conclusion: 该基础设施一旦开发成熟，可降低几何分析中数学结果验证的成本。

Abstract: We present an AI-assisted Lean 4 formalization of the Poincaré conjecture. The project began with limited reusable formal infrastructure for the geometric analysis behind the proof. To organize this work, we combined a proof blueprint prepared by mathematicians with explicit milestone statements. These milestones enabled parallel agent work and gave mathematicians clear points to locate blockers and provide effective mathematical guidance. Our analysis identifies the human interventions and organizational choices behind this workflow. The project provides a starting point toward reusable infrastructure for future formalization projects; such infrastructure, once developed, could eventually reduce the cost of verifying mathematical results in geometric analysis.

</details>


### [146] [Learning a Ranking from Human Feedback in Log-Concave Random Utility Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07973)
*Diego Alovisetti,Marco Mussi,Alberto Maria Metelli*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究在两种人类反馈类型下恢复固定物品集按未知效用排序的问题，并分析达到ε-精确排序所需的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互式排序中，全排名反馈与仅胜者反馈的样本效率差异尚未被充分理解，需要建立下界并设计匹配算法。

Method: 采用随机效用模型与对数凹噪声，分别针对全排名反馈和仅胜者反馈建立最坏情况下样本复杂度下界，并开发无需噪声分布知识（仅需方差上界）的匹配算法。

Result: 两种反馈类型下均得到匹配下界（至多对数因子）的算法；仅胜者反馈的样本复杂度依赖于物品集上的最小获胜概率。

Conclusion: 仅胜者反馈下的排序问题本质上更难，其样本复杂度与最小获胜概率直接相关。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the ranking of a fixed set of items according to their unknown numerical utilities. At each interaction with the environment, a learner presents the item set to a human and receives comparative feedback of two types. Under full-ranking feedback, each interaction reveals a noisy ranking of all items, whereas under winner-only feedback, it reveals only the item ranked first. In both settings, we model human feedback using a random utility model with log-concave noise and study the number of observations needed to recover an $ε$-accurate ranking with high probability. This novel criterion tolerates ordering errors only between items whose utilities differ by less than $ε$. For both feedback types, we establish worst-case sample-complexity lower bounds and develop algorithms that match these bounds up to logarithmic factors. Neither algorithm requires knowledge of the noise distribution, while only requiring an upper bound on its variance. Our results show that the ranking problem under winner-only feedback is intrinsically harder by exposing the sample complexity dependence on the minimum winning probability across the item set.

</details>


### [147] [A Broader Look at Model Merging: Rethinking Implicit Regularization Induced by Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07990)
*Sin-Han Yang,Shih-Cheng Huang,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Shao-Hua Sun,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文质疑模型合并中系数搜索带来的隐式正则化，发现直接优化合并权重可显著提升多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法通过在任务特定权重更新张成的子空间内搜索系数来寻找最优线性组合，这种系数搜索引入了隐式正则化。作者希望探究该正则化是否真正有益。

Method: 取消对系数的子空间限制，直接优化合并模型的权重；并在多种架构、领域以及每类仅一个样本的极端数据受限场景下进行实证比较，同时分析不同使用额外数据集的策略。

Result: 无正则化的直接权重优化显著提升了常见合并方法的多任务性能；直接优化预训练模型权重甚至优于部分现有合并方法。分析表明，子空间外存在更优的多任务权重，且可通过多种方法找到。

Conclusion: 应重新审视现有模型合并流程，鼓励更广泛地探索权重空间，并重新考虑任务算术所诱导的隐式正则化。

Abstract: Model merging aims to build a multi-task model cheaply by combining the weights of individual task-specific models. To perform well across multiple tasks, most existing merging methods use an additional dataset to find the coefficients for the best linear combination of task-specific weight updates. However, we identify an implicit regularization in this standard practice: searching over coefficients restricts the candidate models to a subspace spanned by task-specific weight updates. In this work, we investigate whether this regularization is actually useful. Surprisingly, empirical results show that optimizing merged-model weights without this regularization significantly boosts the performance of common merging methods across multiple architectures, domains, and even in an extremely data-limited scenario where only one instance is available per class. Moreover, directly optimizing the pretrained model weights even outperforms some existing merging methods. Analysis shows that better multi-task weights exist outside the subspace and can be found using multiple methods. We study different strategies for using the additional dataset, discussing their practical use and implications for model merging. Overall, this work calls for revisiting the existing model-merging pipeline, motivating a broader exploration of the weight space and a reconsideration of the implicit regularization induced by task arithmetic.

</details>


### [148] [Recursive Game Creator: An Agentic Product-Level Experience-Oriented Game Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08621)
*Jiajun Chen,Haoyu Wu,Mingda Jia,Xihui Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出递归式游戏创作框架 Recursive Game Creator，以体验为导向，将智能体游戏开发从原型推进到可玩性更高的成品游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏设计智能体虽能生成可玩游戏，但程序正确并不保证玩家获得良好体验，因此需要面向体验的智能体游戏开发方法。

Method: 围绕 Designer、Builder、Player、Reviewer 四个组件组织递归开发：Designer 将用户指令与 Reviewer 反馈转为详细计划；Builder 生成候选游戏；编码原生 Player 通过程序接口创建并执行可复用策略，高效采集多样玩法轨迹，缓解基于 GUI 的慢速采集带来的评估偏差；Reviewer 使用轨迹指标推断玩家偏好，并结合视觉证据与显式文本偏好按游戏特定标准评估；Reviewer 接受更优版本并提供改进意见，形成递归闭环。

Result: 在 GameCraft-Bench 上取得 77.89 的最优综合性能；在 GameASG-Bench 上达到 53.2% 的严格任务成功率，较同模型基线提升 34.1%，并在对比方法中取得最高的平均运行时检查通过率 93.4%；用户研究显示更长的游玩时长与更高评分。

Conclusion: 该体验导向的递归框架能有效提升智能体生成游戏的体验质量与任务表现，代码即将发布。

Abstract: Recent game design agents have made substantial progress in generating playable games. However, program correctness does not ensure an enjoyable experience for players. We present Recursive Game Creator, an experience-oriented harness to advance agentic game development from rough game prototypes into entertaining games. Recursive Game Creator organizes recursive development around four components: Designer, Builder, Player, and Reviewer. The Designer translates user instructions and Reviewer's feedback into detailed plans. The Builder turns these plans into candidate games. The coding-native Player creates and executes reusable policies through programmatic interfaces to efficiently collect diverse gameplay trajectories, mitigating evaluation bias caused by slow GUI-based collection. The Reviewer uses carefully designed trajectory-based metrics to induce player preferences, integrating with visual evidence and explicit textual preferences to evaluate games against game-specific criteria. Finally, the Reviewer accepts the better version and provides improvement reviews for the next round, closing the recursive loop. Our method achieves state-of-the-art overall performance of 77.89 on GameCraft-Bench. On GameASG-Bench, it achieves a strict task success rate of 53.2%, a 34.1% improvement over the same-model baseline, and the highest mean runtime-check pass rate at 93.4% among compared methods. A user study shows longer playtime and higher ratings. Code is coming soon.

</details>


### [149] [Detecting a Shift Is Not Enough: Exact Minimax Limits of Linear Representation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08069)
*Anuar Aimoldin,Yankai Chen,Ayana Mussabayeva,Xue Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文将数据源间的均值漂移移除建模为统计决策问题，推导出线性映射在硬失真预算下的精确有限样本极小极大风险，并揭示了检测与修复之间的样本复杂度差距，同时给出了标准线性概念擦除器的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值漂移易于检测但难以在不显著改变数据表示的情况下移除，现有方法缺乏对其在新鲜数据上残余漂移的精确理论刻画。

Method: 从配对校准测量的含噪差异中学习一个线性映射，对两个数据源同时施加硬失真预算，推导有限样本极小极大风险，并分析投影去除均值校准差异的估计器。

Result: 精确极小极大风险为$(d-k)\mathbb{E}[1/(d+2J)]$，其中$J\sim\mathrm{Pois}(\kappa/2)$；检测漂移仅需$\kappa\gg\sqrt d$，而移除固定比例漂移且保持恒定失真需$\kappa\asymp d$；该公式适用于MP、SAL、LEACE等标准线性擦除器，并在非高斯共享内容、各向异性噪声下保持稳健，选择性弃权无法弥补差距。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了检测-修复差距，可判断校正不足时需要更好的方法、更多记录还是更多参与者，并在配对临床与可穿戴睡眠EEG数据上验证了公式对设备漂移的预测。

Abstract: A mean shift between two data sources can be easy to detect but hard to remove without substantially changing their representations. We cast its removal as a statistical decision problem: from noisy differences between paired calibration measurements in $\mathbb{R}^d$, learn one linear map, applied to both sources under a hard distortion budget, that leaves as little of the shift as possible on fresh data. We derive the exact finite-sample minimax risk over all such maps, $(d-k) \mathbb{E}[1/(d+2J)]$ with $J\sim\mathrm{Pois}(κ/2)$, where the budget allows deleting $k$ directions and $κ$ is the calibration signal-to-noise ratio. Projecting out the mean calibration difference attains it without knowing $κ$ or the noise scale. This exposes a detection-repair gap: detecting the shift needs only $κ\gg\sqrt d$, whereas removing a fixed fraction of it at constant distortion needs $κ\asymp d$, as for estimating its direction. Standard linear concept erasers (MP, SAL, LEACE) remove the same calibration difference, so the formula gives, before fitting, exactly how much shift they leave on fresh data and how much calibration a target requires. The limit is robust: pairing keeps it exact for non-Gaussian shared content, the projection keeps its guarantee under anisotropic noise, and selective abstention cannot close the gap. On paired clinical and wearable sleep EEG, where differences between participants act as calibration noise, the formula predicts the device shift left in new participants, and more recordings per person soon stop helping. Together, these results tell whether a correction that falls short needs a better method, more recordings, or more participants.

</details>


### [150] [ParanoiaEval: Benchmarking Unnecessary Defensive Work in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08662)
*Hanjun Luo,Xiucheng Zhang,Zhuoning Xu,Zhimu Huang,Yingbin Jin,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出首个统一评估编码智能体风险处置能力的基准 ParanoiaEval，基于 ATMA 框架构建 200 个证据控制任务对，揭示风险处置是与任务能力独立的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体自主承担实际工作后，判断其风险处置是否恰当至关重要；现有研究从分散视角评估相关行为，缺乏统一框架来系统化这些行为。

Method: 基于软件工程风险管理的 Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance 框架，将其 4 种基本处置操作化到编码智能体场景；构建 ParanoiaEval 基准，包含 200 个仓库级任务对，每对仅在定义处置的证据上不同；提出风险处置违规与证据响应性专用指标，并采用人工校准的智能体评判器进行可靠评估。

Result: 在 8 个代表性模型上的大规模实验及事后人类研究显示：(I) 即使有明确证据，11.2%-58.7% 的运行出现不必要的风险处置，且不同智能体配置差异显著；(II) 更强任务能力并不保证更恰当的风险处置，处置违规严重损害开发者体验；(III) 智能体表现出与既有风险管理发现一致的系统性模式。

Conclusion: 风险处置应被视为独立的能力维度；人类风险管理实践中的知识可指导该能力的诊断与改进。

Abstract: As coding agents increasingly undertake real-world work autonomously, judging whether their risk treatments are warranted has become important. Existing work evaluates related agent behaviors from separate perspectives, but lacks a systematic framework for unifying these behaviors. To bridge this gap, we introduce ParanoiaEval, the first benchmark for unified evaluation of risk-treatment capabilities in coding agents. Grounded in the well-established Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance framework in software engineering risk management, ParanoiaEval operationalizes its 4 fundamental treatments for coding-agent settings and contains 200 evidence-controlled repository-level task pairs, each differing only in treatment-defining evidence. We further introduce dedicated metrics for risk-treatment violations and evidence responsiveness, using a human-calibrated agentic judge for reliable evaluation. Large-scale experiments on 8 representative models and a post-hoc human study reveal that (I) unnecessary risk treatment occurs in 11.2%-58.7% of runs despite explicit evidence, with substantial variation across agent configurations; (II) stronger task capability does not ensure more appropriate risk treatment, while treatment violations substantially harm developers' experience, establishing risk treatment as an independent capability dimension; and (III) agents exhibit systematic patterns consistent with established risk-management findings, suggesting that knowledge from human practice can guide the diagnosis and improvement of this capability.

</details>


### [151] [ScienceClaw: Benchmarking Continual Self-Evolution of AI-for-Science Agents Across the Natural and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08691)
*Mingda Zhang,Wenjin Liu,Tiesunlong Shen,Zikai Xiao,Zhenghong Lin,Qing Xu,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang,Haoran Luo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ScienceClaw框架，将科学自动化中的程序自进化形式化为固定参数程序自进化，并设计ScienceClaw-Eval评估其跨任务持续改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体加速科学自动化，但验证过的执行很少转化为持久的程序级改进，且现有评估未在自然与社会科学顺序任务中考察这一过程。

Method: ScienceClaw通过多轮交互修复可执行工作流，将重执行验证的失败-成功轨迹转化为关联的Skill和Operator候选，仅当源任务重放复现修复且独立科学任务改善时保留更新；ScienceClaw-Eval覆盖23个学科，通过顺序流和独立重置评估测量科学正确性、进化增益、保持、跨数据集迁移和进化成本。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果，仅说明代码已开源。

Conclusion: ScienceClaw为科学自动化中的程序自进化提供了统一框架与评估基准，但需实验验证其实际效果。

Abstract: Large language model agents are accelerating scientific automation, yet verified executions rarely become persistent program-level improvements, and existing evaluations do not examine this process across sequential tasks in both the natural and social sciences. We formalize ScienceClaw as fixed-parameter program self-evolution that unifies task solving, scientific verification, and program updates. ScienceClaw-Eval spans 23 disciplines and measures scientific correctness, evolutionary gain, retention, cross-dataset transfer, and evolution cost through sequential streams and independent reset evaluation. Our framework repairs executable workflows through multi-turn interaction, converts re-execution-verified failure--success trajectories into linked Skill and Operator candidates, and retains an update only when source-task replay reproduces the repair and independent scientific tasks improve. Code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/ScienceClaw.

</details>


### [152] [Do LLMs Act on What They Know? From Partner Representations to Cooperative Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08129)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Jinnyeong Yang,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究在受控的Hanabi衍生环境中，考察LLM接收方在与未知伙伴合作时如何适应对方的提示约定，发现线性探针能较好地解码意图约定，但接收决策并未一致遵循发送方约定；提供外部计算的动作建议比规则陈述更能提升合作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与不熟悉的伙伴合作需要适应事先未知的沟通约定，而LLM是否以及如何利用可获得的伙伴信息做出接收决策尚不明确。

Method: 构建受控的Hanabi衍生环境，使用脚本化提示生成、LLM控制接收决策并冻结模型权重；对八个LLM进行线性探针分析，比较探针预测与真实约定以规则或外部动作建议形式呈现的效果，并在Qwen3-8B中进行匹配状态陈述反转案例研究及激活转移实验。

Result: 线性探针恢复意图约定的准确率显著高于目标约定，但接收选择与发送方约定不一致；规则陈述带来有限且依赖模型的变化，动作翻译平均增益更大；匹配状态反转显示对动作建议的敏感度远高于规则陈述；来自oracle动作和非oracle提示重述供体的激活转移提升了两类动作的意图准确率，但测试的其他替代方案无法可靠复现这些收益。

Conclusion: 研究区分了约定可解码性、对约定信息的敏感性和合作表现，并指出将可获得的伙伴信息转化为接收决策仍存在局限。

Abstract: Cooperation with unfamiliar partners requires adapting to communication conventions that are not known in advance. We study this problem in a controlled Hanabi-derived environment with scripted hint generation, LLM-controlled receiving decisions, and frozen model weights. Across eight LLMs, linear probes recover intent conventions substantially more accurately than target conventions, yet receiving choices do not consistently agree with the sender's convention. We compare probe-predicted and ground-truth conventions presented either as general rules or as externally computed action recommendations. Rule statements yield modest and model-dependent changes in cooperation, whereas action translation produces larger gains on average. In a Qwen3-8B case study, matched-state statement reversals reveal much greater sensitivity to action recommendations than to rule statements. Activation transfers from oracle-action and non-oracle hint-restatement donors improve intent accuracy on both action classes, but the tested alternatives do not reliably reproduce these benefits. Together, these results distinguish convention decodability, sensitivity to convention information, and cooperative performance, and highlight limitations in turning available partner information into receiving decisions.

</details>


### [153] [Structure-Aware Graph Abstention for Reliable Selective Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08322)
*Jianxiang Xie,Belal Alsinglawi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文将多变量预测中的选择性预测拆分为实例级合理性与关系一致性两个可靠性轴，并提出基于稀疏图和结构能量的结构门控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性预测门控（如TEM）对整个预测打分，对多变量输出而言轨迹可能看似合理却违反变量间依赖关系，因此需要单独度量关系一致性。

Method: 学习稀疏图并定义Dirichlet式结构能量E_struct，通过误差加权图正则化和分数-误差对齐进行训练，在保留覆盖率预算下与TEM等门控比较。

Result: 在7个长时程基准和4个骨干网络上，结构门控在匹配覆盖率下常降低选择性MSE，尤其在跨变量结构更具信息量的基准上增益最大；但增益不普遍。

Conclusion: 结构门控提供了与实例级合理性互补的弃权信号，可作为选择性预测的额外可靠性维度。

Abstract: Selective forecasting abstains on high-risk test windows under a retained-coverage budget. Existing gates such as TEM (Brusokas et al., 2025) score each forecast as a whole; for multivariate outputs, trajectories can look plausible while violating dependencies among variables. We treat instance-level plausibility and relational consistency as distinct reliability axes and operationalize the latter via a learned sparse graph and a Dirichlet-style structural energy E_struct, trained with error-weighted graph regularization and score-error alignment. On seven long-horizon benchmarks and four backbones, structural gating often reduces selective MSE versus TEM at matched coverage, with the largest gains where cross-variable structure appears more informative in our benchmarks; gains are not universal, indicating a complementary abstention signal. Table 1 is a Protocol A ranking diagnostic (seed 2024); three-seed deployable Protocol B on an aligned subset is in Table 3 (full validation-to-test grids: Appendix A).

</details>


### [154] [Uncertainty Quantification Is Indispensable for Reliable Connectome-Based Graph Learning: A Narrative Review and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08353)
*Mansooreh Pakravan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文综述了连接组图学习中的不确定性量化（UQ）框架，并通过案例研究揭示了未校准预测的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在连接组诊断分类中表现出潜力，但确定性模型会抑制噪声和模型模糊性，导致过度自信预测；UQ在体素级分割中广泛应用，但在连接组图学习中尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 进行综合性叙述综述，梳理神经影像流程中偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的来源，回顾主流UQ范式（贝叶斯近似、集成方法、证据学习、保形预测）；案例研究使用SUDMEX CONN数据集的动态功能连接矩阵训练时序图注意力网络（GAT），并通过蒙特卡洛dropout进行事后不确定性审计。

Result: GAT对可卡因使用障碍的诊断准确率为80.0%（F1=0.794），但不确定性审计显示严重过度自信（ECE=0.127），误分类受试者的预测置信度高达95%。

Conclusion: 区分度与校准之间的经验差异凸显了深度连接组学中的置信悖论；严格的UQ、校准和选择性预测机制对于在临床神经科学中部署可信的图生物标志物不可或缺。

Abstract: While graph neural networks (GNNs) have shown substantial promise in connectome-based diagnostic classification, deterministic models inevitably suppress pipeline-induced noise and model ambiguities, yielding overconfident predictions. Although uncertainty quantification (UQ) is widely adopted in voxel-level segmentation, its role in connectomic graph learning remains largely unaddressed. This paper presents a comprehensive narrative review of UQ frameworks tailored to connectome graph learning alongside an empirical case study demonstrating the perils of uncalibrated predictions. We delineate sources of aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty across neuroimaging pipelines and review prominent UQ paradigms, from Bayesian approximations and ensemble methods to evidential learning and conformal prediction. In our case study, a temporal Graph Attention Network (GAT) trained on dynamic functional connectivity (dFC) matrices from the SUDMEX CONN dataset achieves 80.0% diagnostic accuracy (F1 = 0.794) for Cocaine Use Disorder. However, a post-hoc uncertainty audit via Monte Carlo dropout reveals severe overconfidence (ECE = 0.127), with misclassified subjects assigned prediction confidences up to 95%. This empirical divergence between discrimination and calibration underscores the confidence paradox in deep connectomics. Our findings establish that rigorous UQ, calibration, and selective prediction mechanisms are indispensable for deploying trustworthy graph-based biomarkers in clinical neuroscience.

</details>


### [155] [Accelerating the Development of PLGA In Situ Forming Depots Through AI-Driven Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08368)
*Pauric Bannigan,Siddarth Chandrasekaran,Brigitte A. G. Lamers,Inge Hermsen,Gary Tom,Riley J. Hickman,Bahar Yeniad,Morgan Fox,Christine Allen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 结合Corbion生物可吸收聚合物库与Intrepid的AI算法ANDROMEDA 1，在15周内制备并表征181种原位形成储库制剂，识别出四种满足黏度与可注射性且具有不同30天释放曲线的先导候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长效注射制剂需同时优化载药量、释放动力学、黏度、可注射性、稳定性等多目标，传统方法难以高效导航多维设计空间。

Method: 采用Corbion的PURASORB聚合物库（含不同分子量商业级及在研新聚合物）与Intrepid专有AI算法ANDROMEDA 1，通过广泛设计空间映射和靶向多目标优化，制备并表征181种载药量为6-12% w/w的原位形成储库制剂。

Result: 在6%、9%、12% w/w载药量下识别出四种先导候选，均满足预设黏度与可注射性标准，并提供不同的30天体外释放曲线；AI算法揭示中等分子量聚合物在缓释与溶液黏度间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 整合聚合物专业知识与AI驱动优化可快速识别差异化制剂候选、聚焦开发空间，并为后续优化和体内评价奠定数据驱动基础。

Abstract: Developing long-acting injectable formulations requires the simultaneous optimization of drug loading, release kinetics, viscosity, injectability, stability and other objectives. To navigate this multidimensional space, Corbion and Intrepid combined Corbion's diverse PURASORB bioresorbable polymer library with Intrepid Labs' proprietary AI algorithm (ANDROMEDA 1) to develop in situ forming depots for a therapeutic peptide. Over approximately 15 weeks, 181 unique formulations spanning drug loadings of 6-12% w/w were prepared and characterized through broad design-space mapping and targeted multi-objective optimization. Four lead candidate formulations were identified at 6%, 9%, and 12% w/w drug loading. Each met the predefined viscosity and injectability criteria while providing distinct 30-day in vitro release profiles. The study evaluated polymers spanning a broad range of molecular weights, including commercially available PURASORB grades and new polymers under development by Corbion to expand its polymer toolbox. ANDROMEDA 1 identified that polymers with intermediate molecular weights provided a favorable balance between sustained release and solution viscosity. Together, these findings demonstrate how integrated polymer expertise and AI-driven optimization can rapidly identify differentiated formulation candidates, focus the development space, and establish a strong data-driven foundation for further optimization and in vivo evaluation.

</details>


### [156] [Decision-Focused Learning in MDPs: An Occupancy Measure Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08384)
*Zihao Zhao,Ashwath K. Karunakaram,Ali Eshragh,Yuexing Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文将决策聚焦学习（DFL）用于马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过将 MDP 重构为基于占用测度的线性规划（LP）并利用转轴算法求闭式梯度，解决了现有 KKT 方法可扩展性差的问题，并进一步用增广拉格朗日、随机行草图与可学习软状态聚合层应对梯度不连续和状态规模瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DFL 方法对 Bellman 方程的 KKT 条件求导，需在所有状态-动作对上求解线性系统，可扩展性受限，难以处理大规模或连续状态 MDP。

Method: 将 MDP 重构为基于占用测度的 LP，其可行域由预测动态诱导；通过转轴算法识别可行多面体的活跃约束，推导闭式梯度；针对活跃约束变化导致的梯度不连续，采用增广拉格朗日代理与约束随机行草图平滑边界跳变；针对 LP 反向代价随状态规模增长，引入可学习软状态聚合层及其函数逼近泛化，将 LP 扩展至大规模有限与连续状态 MDP。

Result: 在多个任务上，所提方法相比基于 KKT 的 DFL 和两阶段基线取得更低遗憾，且计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 基于占用测度 LP 的 DFL 框架结合增广拉格朗日、随机行草图与状态聚合，可有效提升 DFL 在 MDP 中的可扩展性与性能。

Abstract: In this work, we consider decision-focused learning (DFL) for a Markov decision process (MDP), where existing methods differentiate through the KKT conditions of the Bellman equation and require solving a linear system over all state-action pairs, limiting its scalability. We address this by reformulating the MDP as an occupancy measure-based linear program (LP), whose feasible region is induced by predicted dynamics, and we derive a closed-form gradient by identifying the active constraints in the feasible polyhedron via the pivoting algorithm. This occupancy measure-based LP layer raises two challenges: (1) LP's solution gradient is discontinuous when active constraints change, and (2) the LP backward cost still scales with the state size, which is costly for large or continuous state spaces. We address the challenges with an augmented Lagrangian surrogate and smooth the boundary jumps by random row sketching of the constraints, and a learnable soft state-aggregation layer and its function-approximation generalization that scales the LP to large finite and continuous-state MDPs. Across multiple tasks, our methods reach lower regret than KKT-based DFL and two-stage baselines with significantly lower computation cost. The source code for all experiments is available at https://github.com/A-Eshragh/State_Aggregation_Project.

</details>


### [157] [FlowCF: Sparse Counterfactual Explanations for Mixed-Type Tabular Data using Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08537)
*Emmanouil Panagiotou,Eirini Ntoutsi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FlowCF，一种基于流匹配的模型无关反事实解释生成方法，通过稀疏传输与门控网络优化，显著提升数值特征的稀疏性和接近性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在数值特征上稀疏性和接近性不足，无论是模型无关/摊销方法还是完全访问模型的梯度方法。

Method: 将反事实生成建模为从事实类到目标类的稀疏传输，用流匹配求解，并扩展至混合特征类型（混合流算子）；利用传输几何，通过门控网络最小化被改变特征数来优化稀疏性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，FlowCF取得最佳数值稀疏性和接近性：改变29%的数值特征（最佳基线为89%），位移量小70%，其他指标相当。

Conclusion: FlowCF能生成更稀疏、更接近的反事实解释，且保持其他期望性质，验证了流匹配与门控机制在混合特征反事实生成中的有效性。

Abstract: In the field of Explainable AI (XAI), counterfactual (CF) explanations interpret a model's decision by suggesting the changes to the input that would lead to a more favourable outcome. To be useful in practice, such an explanation should change few features and change them as little as possible, properties known as sparsity and proximity. We observe that existing methods remain limited in this respect, especially for numerical features, whether they are model-agnostic and amortised, or gradient-based with full access to the model. In this paper, we propose FlowCF, a model-agnostic generative method that frames CF generation as sparse transport from the factual to the target class. We solve this transport with flow matching, which we extend to mixed feature types with a novel mixed flow operator, and exploit the resulting geometry to optimise for sparsity through a gating network that minimises the number of features the transport changes. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that FlowCF produces the best numerical sparsity and proximity, changing 29% of the numerical features where the best baseline changes 89%, at 70% smaller displacement, while remaining comparable on the other desiderata.

</details>


### [158] [Singular Value Decomposition: A Geometric Rediscovery, Where Proofs Become Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08565)
*Paul Agron*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度重新发现奇异值分解，并论证该构造是许多机器学习方法背后的机制，如主成分分析、核方法和PageRank，不仅结果可迁移，证明本身也可作为过程运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SVD引入方式依赖于谱定理，虽正确但缺乏启发性；作者希望从更直观的几何角度（椭圆映射）出发，揭示SVD与机器学习算法之间的深层联系。

Method: 第一部分反转论证顺序：从线性映射将单位圆映射为椭圆出发，通过旋转框架追踪图像偏离垂直的程度，利用符号变化找到垂直方向，即最大拉伸方向；最大化拉伸并递归推广到n维，从而按顺序得到奇异值，并证明谱定理。第二部分将每个构造应用于机器学习：最大化-递归对应幂法和PageRank；定位最大值的引理对应梯度下降的停止规则；A^T A与A A^T的对偶性对应核PCA中的传输。

Result: 通过几何构造自然导出SVD，并证明谱定理；同时将构造与机器学习算法一一对应，并说明每个联系的边界和适用条件。

Conclusion: SVD的几何构造不仅是理解椭圆映射的工具，更是机器学习中多种算法的统一机制；该工作为SVD和机器学习方法提供了直观且可操作的几何视角，适合具备二年级数学基础的学习者通过手算示例掌握。

Abstract: This article is a geometric rediscovery of the singular value decomposition, with a further claim: the construction it builds is the machinery behind much of machine learning. The same argument that answers an idle question about ellipses is the algorithm behind principal component analysis, kernel methods, and PageRank, and it is not only the results that transfer but the proofs themselves, run as procedures.
  The usual introduction states $A = UΣV^T$ and justifies it via the spectral theorem applied to $A^T A$. This is correct but unilluminating, since it assumes a powerful theorem to reach a result that is, in the end, about ellipses. Part I reverses the order. A linear map sends the unit circle to an ellipse; one asks which input directions map to its axes, and finds, example after example, that they are perpendicular. In the plane this can be watched: rotate a frame, track how far its images are from perpendicular, and a sign change forces a frame where they are exactly perpendicular, which is also where the map stretches hardest. Maximizing the stretch and recursing generalizes this to n dimensions, with singular values falling out in order, and the construction proves the spectral theorem rather than assuming it.
  Part II puts each construction to work: maximize-and-recurse becomes the power method and PageRank; the lemma locating the maximizer becomes the stopping rule of gradient descent; the duality between $A^T A$ and $A A^T$ becomes the transport at the heart of kernel PCA. Each connection is stated with its boundary, saying what the decomposition supplies and where another idea takes over. Prerequisites are the standard sophomore sequence, and the worked examples are small enough to check by hand.

</details>


### [159] [Multi-Label Perceptual Bug Detection in Video Games using Deep Learning on Gameplay Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08593)
*Nahian Rifaat,Felix Morosov,Loutfouz Zaman*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的多标签感知缺陷检测模型ResNet-BiLSTM，用于视频游戏自动化缺陷检测，在基准数据集上F1分数达85.78%，并发布了一个包含77,969个视频片段的新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频游戏缺陷检测依赖人工测试，成本高且耗时；现有自动化工具难以在同一视频帧中检测多种感知缺陷，且可用工具稀少，难以应对真实场景。

Method: 提出ResNet-BiLSTM模型进行多标签感知缺陷检测，并与Inflated 3D ConvNet、3D ResNet等视频分类模型进行对比；同时构建新数据集，包含77,969个视频片段、约120万帧，覆盖不同游戏类型，同一视频帧可含5类缺陷组合。

Result: ResNet-BiLSTM在基准数据集上取得85.78%的F1分数；结果表明时间依赖建模有助于提高基于视频的缺陷检测准确性。

Conclusion: 多标签感知缺陷检测研究可减少视频游戏人工测试的资源消耗，新数据集为该领域提供了基准，时间依赖建模对视频缺陷检测有效。

Abstract: Traditional approaches for automated bug detection in video games, such as manual testing, can be beneficial for the improvement of quality assurance, but they can be expensive and time-consuming. The scarce number of tools available to detect multiple perceptual bugs in the same video frame introduces detection challenges for automated bug detection tools in real-world scenarios. We propose a deep learning model for multi-label perceptual bug detection and compare it against video classification models such as Inflated 3D ConvNet and 3D ResNet. Our proposed model, ResNet-BiLSTM, achieved an F1 score of 85.78% on the benchmark dataset. Our results demonstrated that temporal dependency modelling is beneficial for accurate video-based bug detection. We believe this work with multi-label perceptual bug detection on gameplay videos will help save resources spent on manual testing workloads in video games. Furthermore, we introduce a new dataset with multi-label perceptual bugs in this work. The dataset contains 77,969 video clips across different genres of games with approximately 1.2 million frames, containing combinations from 5 classes of bugs in the same video frame.

</details>


### [160] [On the Computational Tractability of Robust Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08740)
*Vanessa Kosoy,Vinayak Pathak*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究不精确（稳健）赌博机中不可实现学习的计算可行性，找到一个多项式时间可解的特例，并证明其若干小扩展是NP难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可实现学习通常用不可知保证处理，但在监督学习之外难以获得；不精确赌博机虽已有Θ(√T)遗憾界，却缺乏计算保证。

Method: 识别一个特殊情形，设计多项式时间学习算法；并通过归约证明该特例的若干小推广是NP难的。

Result: 该特例存在多项式时间学习器，达到Õ(√T)遗憾；其小推广为NP难，表明该特例处于可处理边界。

Conclusion: 该工作为不可实现学习问题的高效算法研究提供了小步进展，对AI对齐问题具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Learning when the environment does not belong to the learner's hypothesis class is typically handled using agnostic learning guarantees. However, for anything beyond supervised learning, agnostic guarantees are difficult to come by. Recently, imprecise bandits (Kosoy, 2025) (later renamed to robust bandits in Appel and Kosoy, 2025) were introduced as another approach to unrealizable learning in the bandits setting and a $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret learner was shown for a large class. However, no computational guarantees were provided. In this paper we identify a special case that admits a polynomial-time learner with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret. We also show that several small generalizations of this special case are NP-hard thus indicating that the special case is at the boundary of what is tractable. It has been recently suggested (Kosoy, 2018) that computationally efficient learners for unrealizable learning problems are crucial for solving the AI alignment problem. This work is a small step in that direction.

</details>


### [161] [Conformal Prediction Sets Quantify Information Gain: A Theoretical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08785)
*Kevin Zhang,Stephen Bates*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文为共形预测中预测集大小作为不确定性度量提供了信息论基础，定义了基于集合大小与覆盖率的广义信息度量，并建立了其与香农互信息的积分表示及数据处理不等式等理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测常以预测集大小作为不确定性的启发式度量，但这种解释缺乏信息论依据，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用面向集合值预测的决策论熵泛化，引入基于共形预测集大小和覆盖率的广义信息度量族，证明香农互信息可由这些度量精确积分表示，并在标准分类设定下推导集合大小缩减与信息增益之间的夹逼关系及数据处理不等式（含有限样本校准与模型误差项），最后在11个分类任务上验证并开展贪心特征选择实验。

Result: 理论上，互信息可精确积分表示；集合大小缩减被校准相关的度量族成员夹逼并满足数据处理不等式。实验在11个分类设定中验证了理论，并发现集合大小缩减与香农互信息在贪心特征选择中可给出不同的特征排序。

Conclusion: 该工作从形式上把共形预测与经典信息论量联系起来，为将预测集大小缩减用作信息增益度量提供了理论依据，同时提示其与互信息在特征选择等应用中的排序差异。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a popular tool for uncertainty quantification that outputs prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees. While prediction set size is commonly used as a heuristic measure of uncertainty, the information-theoretic basis for this interpretation remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide such a foundation using a decision-theoretic generalization of entropy tailored to set-valued prediction. In particular, we introduce a family of generalized information measures based on the size and coverage of conformal prediction sets. Notably, Shannon mutual information admits an exact integral representation in terms of these measures. We then show that, in standard classification settings, the reduction in conformal set size from additional information (i) is sandwiched between calibration-dependent members of this family and (ii) obeys a data processing inequality, both up to finite-sample calibration and model error terms. Together, our results formally relate conformal prediction to classical information-theoretic quantities and justify using set-size reduction as an information gain metric. Empirically, we validate our theory across 11 classification settings and show that set-size reduction and Shannon mutual information can rank features differently in a greedy feature selection experiment.

</details>


### [162] [What Does Fréchet Distance Measure? A Directional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05518)
*Yunghee Lee,Jaeyeon Kim*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 论文提出方向Fréchet距离，通过将最优传输位移投影到特定方向来分解Fréchet距离，从而提升其可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet距离（如图像的FID和视频的FVD）将生成分布与参考分布的差异总结为单一标量，但该标量视角掩盖了差异的来源，例如扩散采样步数增加时ImageReward提升而FID反而变差，这种不一致促使作者挖掘差异的具体方向。

Method: 定义方向Fréchet距离为最优传输位移在给定方向上的期望平方投影，并在图像、视频和蛋白质案例中分解总体距离，识别出少数可解释的方向。

Result: 实验发现少数可解释方向解释了大部分Fréchet距离；利用这些方向结合CLIP嵌入的语义概念解释了FID的增加，量化了FVD对逐帧外观的偏向，并重新审视了Protein FID的解释。

Conclusion: 方向Fréchet距离能有效提升Fréchet距离的可解释性，揭示不同模态下距离的主要驱动因素，代码已开源。

Abstract: The Fréchet distance is a de facto standard for evaluating generative models across domains, appearing as FID for images and FVD for videos. It summarizes the discrepancy between generated and reference distributions in a single scalar, with lower values typically interpreted as better generation quality. However, this scalar view can obscure what drives the comparison. For example, in COCO dataset, increasing the number of diffusion sampling steps improves ImageReward scores yet worsens (increases) FID. Motivated by this mismatch, we seek to make the Fréchet distance more interpretable by uncovering where the discrepancy lies. To this end, we introduce directional Fréchet distance, the expected squared projection of the optimal transport displacement onto a given direction. Across our image, video, and protein case studies, we find that a small number of interpretable directions account for much of the distance. We use these directions to explain the FID increase in terms of semantic concepts represented by CLIP embeddings, quantify FVD's bias toward per-frame appearance, and revisit the interpretation of Protein FID. We open-source our codebase at https://github.com/yhlee-add/directional-fd.

</details>


<div id='Training'></div>

# Training [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [TEMPEST: Temporal Embeddings for Scalable Driver Identification via Angular Margin Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06855)
*Kyle Musgrove,Dylan B. Lewis,Sarah Powers,Emma J. Reid,Hector Santos-Villalobos*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出TEMPEST模型，用ArcFace损失训练时序卷积网络，实现可扩展的驾驶员身份识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三元组损失模型在车队规模增大时判别性能急剧下降，且易过拟合会话特定模式，难以满足动态注册需求。

Method: 基于时序卷积网络，采用加性角度间隔（ArcFace）损失在归一化角度空间强制全局类别分离，将60秒多模态驾驶窗口映射为96维紧凑嵌入，无需重训练即可动态注册。

Result: 在45名驾驶员数据集上，Rank-1准确率91.71%，比最佳经典模型高17.9个百分点，比最强三元组基线高58.4个百分点；驾驶员从10增至45时性能仅降4.3个百分点，而基线降22和32.5个百分点；在KIA Soul数据集上会话内和跨会话分别高7.3和14.3个百分点；模型仅720K参数、2.80 MB、50轮收敛。

Conclusion: TEMPEST为可扩展行为驾驶员生物识别建立了严格、可复现的基线。

Abstract: Scalable driver identification requires embedding models that maintain discriminative performance as fleet size grows, yet existing triplet-loss formulations degrade rapidly with driver pool size and overfit to session-specific patterns under rigorous temporal evaluation. We introduce TEMPEST, a Temporal Convolutional Network embedding model trained with an additive angular margin (ArcFace) loss that enforces global class-level separation in a normalized angular space. TEMPEST maps 60-second multimodal driving windows to compact 96-dimensional embeddings, supporting truly dynamic enrollment without any retraining or classifier refitting. Under rigorous temporal evaluation on a 45-driver dataset, TEMPEST achieves 91.71% Rank-1 accuracy, outperforming the best classical model by 17.9 pp and the strongest triplet-loss baseline by 58.4 pp. TEMPEST degrades by only 4.3 pp when growing the subject pool from 10 to 45 drivers, compared to 22 pp and 32.5 pp for supervised and unsupervised triplet-loss baselines, and its cross-session advantage is corroborated on the public KIA Soul dataset, where it outperforms the best classical model by 7.3 pp within-session and 14.3 pp cross-session. With 720K parameters, a 2.80 MB footprint, and 50-epoch convergence, TEMPEST establishes a rigorous, reproducible baseline for scalable behavioral driver biometric identification.

</details>


### [164] [Metonymic Circuits for Abstract Concept Grounding in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06928)
*Jing Ding,Ziqiao Ma,Jiayuan Mao,Joyce Chai,Freda Shi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究在训练数据缺乏直接指称证据时，视觉Transformer如何将抽象概念（如愤怒）进行具身接地，提出并验证了一种转喻接地机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注具体概念接地，而抽象概念（如情绪）在视觉上缺乏直接指称，其接地机制尚不明确，因此需要探索视觉模型如何利用有限证据将抽象语义与视觉信号关联。

Method: 在CLIP和DINO视觉编码器上应用Transcoders，恢复可关联具体概念语义标签的中间特征，并追踪其在抽象概念识别电路中的贡献；使用精心整理的图标数据集进行实验，并结合因果干预验证中间特征的功能性。

Result: 实验揭示了结构化的转喻电路：早期层以感知基元为主，物体类锚点先于抽象目标出现；包含渲染文本的图像则招募一条独特的感知到文本路径。因果干预进一步证实转喻中间特征在抽象概念接地中具有功能作用。

Conclusion: 视觉Transformer通过转喻机制，借助具体、可解释的锚点概念（如火）桥接视觉信号与抽象语义（如愤怒），且该机制在因果上对抽象概念接地至关重要。

Abstract: We study how Vision Transformers ground abstract concepts (e.g., angry) when training data provide limited direct referential evidence. We hypothesize a metonymic grounding mechanism in which abstract predictions are driven by concrete, interpretable anchor concepts (e.g., fire) that bridge visual signals to abstract semantics. By applying Transcoders on CLIP and DINO vision encoders, we recover intermediate features that can be associated with semantic labels for more concrete concepts, and trace their contributions in circuits underlying abstract concept recognition. Experiments on a carefully curated icon dataset reveal structured metonymic circuits, in which perceptual primitives dominate early layers and object-like anchors precede abstract targets. Images containing rendered text instead recruit a distinct perceptual-to-textual route. Causal interventions further validate that metonymic intermediates are functionally involved in grounding abstract concepts.

</details>


### [165] [Event-Driven ML Pipeline Orchestration for Manufacturing: An AWS Industry Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06890)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Thomas Cook,Fredaljohn Rohrbaugh,Joseph E. Hernandez,Chris Couch*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文报告了在汽车制造业中运行事件驱动云基础设施以持续进行机器学习训练三年的行业经验，系统编排GPU加速训练产品专用模型对，实现72-78%的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在汽车制造中，需要持续训练产品专用模型对（物理预测模型和强化学习控制策略），而传统常开GPU基础设施成本高昂。

Method: 架构结合Amazon ECS与EC2 GPU容量提供者、基于SQS的消息传递与死信队列、以及实施准入控制的Lambda调度器；使用ECS Fargate上的Conductor编排器按周计划启动依赖感知的再训练链；整个基础设施用模块化Terraform编码并多账户分离。

Result: 从40000+生产训练作业中，相比常开GPU基础设施，成本降低72-78%；离散事件模拟证实准入控制是必要的（朴素调度会丢失65%的作业），且队列排空匹配AWS Step Functions延迟同时消除每作业启动开销。

Conclusion: 提供了经验教训，并将模拟器和Terraform模块骨架作为开源工件发布。

Abstract: We present an industry experience report on three years of operating an event-driven cloud infrastructure for continuous machine learning training in automotive manufacturing. Our system orchestrates GPU-accelerated training of product-specialized model pairs, a physics prediction model and a reinforcement-learning control policy, across multiple plants, coordinating long-running GPU workloads triggered by manufacturing events. The architecture combines Amazon ECS with EC2 GPU capacity providers, SQS-based messaging with dead-letter queues, and an admission-controlled Lambda dispatcher that enforces cluster concurrency limits. A Conductor orchestrator on ECS Fargate initiates dependency-aware retraining chains on a weekly schedule. The entire infrastructure is codified in modular Terraform with multi-account separation. From 40000+ production training jobs we report a 72-78% cost reduction versus always-on GPU infrastructure. A discrete-event simulation confirms that admission control is necessary (naive dispatch loses 65% of jobs) and that queue-draining matches AWS Step Functions latency while eliminating per-job startup overhead. We provide lessons learned and release the simulator and Terraform module skeletons as open-source artifacts.

</details>


### [166] [Near-Optimal Sample Complexity for Recursive Entropic Risk Reinforcement Learning with a Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06931)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文分析了在递归熵风险偏好下有限折扣MDP中基于生成模型的Q值迭代的样本复杂度，改进了对有效视界的指数依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作对风险敏感Q值迭代的上界与下界在有效视界上存在指数差距，需要更精细的分析来缩小这一差距。

Method: 对基于模型的插件式风险敏感Q值迭代（MB-RS-QVI）进行精细分析，推导了学习最优Q值函数和ε-最优策略的(ε,δ)-PAC保证。

Result: 所得到的界在|β|/(1-γ)的指数依赖上匹配现有下界，同时在S、A、ε和|β|上仅差对数因子，消除了指数差距，仅剩有效视界的多项式差距。

Conclusion: 该分析移除了先前上界与下界之间的指数差距，将差距缩小到有效视界的多项式级别。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the sample complexities of value and policy learning in finite discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) under recursive entropic risk preferences with risk parameter \(β\neq 0\), assuming access to a generative model of the MDP. We provide a refined analysis of model-based risk-sensitive Q-value iteration (MB-RS-QVI), a plug-in model-based method introduced in prior work, and derive \((\varepsilon,δ)\)-PAC guarantees for both learning the optimal \(Q\)-value function and an \(\varepsilon\)-optimal policy. Our bounds improve the exponential dependence on the effective horizon \(1/(1-γ)\) compared with the best existing guarantees for this setting. In particular, they match the existing lower bounds in their exponential dependence on \(|β|/(1-γ)\), as well as in \(S\), \(A\), \(\varepsilon\), and \(|β|\), up to logarithmic factors. Consequently, our analysis removes the exponential gap between the previously known upper and lower bounds, leaving only a polynomial gap in the effective horizon.

</details>


### [167] [JIVEAdapter: A Multi-Task Additive Low-Rank Adapter via Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07036)
*Sara Abdali,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出JIVEAdapter，一种受JIVE启发的多任务加性低秩适配器，将权重更新分解为跨任务共享的Joint结构与任务特有的Individual结构，并自适应分配秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩适配器多为单任务且以乘性方式表示权重更新，无法显式区分跨任务共享信息与任务特有信息，缺乏可解释性与复用性。

Method: JIVEAdapter将每个权重更新分解为所有任务共享的Joint结构加每任务Individual结构；对Individual施加近正交惩罚以分离共享与特有信号；在共享Joint池与每任务Individual池间自适应分配秩；Joint可一次联合学习或逐任务增量学习，随后冻结并作为新任务先验复用，无需重训共享部分。

Result: 在GLUE与SuperGLUE上以DeBERTaV3-base为基座，在匹配的每任务有效秩下，JIVEAdapter与强单任务及多任务低秩基线相当，且无需MoE等额外模块；当存在相关held-in任务时，冻结的Joint可通过仅重用该任务的Individual并施加廉价逐方向缩放来服务held-out任务，否则训练一个小型新Individual。

Conclusion: JIVEAdapter通过加性Joint-Individual分解实现了共享与任务特化信号的可解释分离，并在多任务参数高效微调中取得有竞争力的性能，同时支持共享知识的冻结复用与低成本迁移。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, yet most low-rank adapters are single-task and represent each weight update multiplicatively, leaving no explicit account of what is shared across tasks and what is task-specific. We introduce JIVEAdapter, a multi-task "additive" low-rank adapter inspired by statistical Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE). JIVEAdapter decomposes every weight update into a Joint structure shared across all tasks plus a per-task Individual structure, penalizes the Individual structures to be near-orthogonal to the Joint so shared and task-specific signal stay "interpretable" and separated, and allocates rank adaptively across a shared Joint pool and a per-task Individual pool. The Joint is learned once, jointly over a task group or incrementally, one task at a time, then frozen and reused as a prior for new tasks without retraining the shared part. On GLUE and SuperGLUE with DeBERTaV3-base, JIVEAdapter is competitive with strong single-task and multi-task low-rank baselines at a matched per-task effective rank, without extra modules such as MoE, and when a related held-in task exists its frozen Joint serves a held-out task by reusing that task's Individual with only a cheap per-direction scale, otherwise training a small new one.

</details>


### [168] [EVFormer: An Egocentric Vision-EMG Bidirectional Attention Model for Bimanual Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06970)
*JiaCheng Ge,SiYu Zhang,ShengJie Li,XinTong Yang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出EVFormer多模态框架，融合RGB帧与前置200ms双侧腕部sEMG估计44个手指和手腕关节角度，在单被试可行性研究中取得优于单模态和晚融合基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角双手姿态估计对虚拟交互、可穿戴控制和康复很重要，但视觉观测常受自遮挡、手手接触和物体操作影响而退化，故需引入肌电信号补充。

Method: EVFormer分别编码视觉空间特征与sEMG时序特征，通过顺序双向交叉注意力实现跨模态信息交互，并用特征级门控融合整合两模态；在单个同步公开EgoEMG记录上，按时间划分训练、验证与测试集进行单被试可行性评估。

Result: 在296个测试样本上，EVFormer平均绝对误差为11.482度，优于视觉单模态、sEMG单模态、晚融合和训练均值基线的13.228–13.610度；相对视觉单模态降低13.20%，相对晚融合降低14.23%，并在五类手势中的四类取得最低误差。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，第一视角视觉与sEMG的特征级交互可提升双手姿态估计；但方法泛化性仍需跨被试、跨记录会话、跨传感器放置及真实交互条件的进一步评估。

Abstract: Egocentric bimanual hand pose estimation is important for virtual interaction, wearable control, and rehabilitation, but visual observations are often degraded by self-occlusion, hand-hand contact, and object manipulation. We propose EVFormer, a multimodal framework that combines the current RGB frame with the preceding 200 ms of bilateral wrist surface electromyography (sEMG) to estimate 44 finger and wrist joint angles. EVFormer separately encodes visual spatial features and sEMG temporal features, enables cross-modal information exchange through sequential bidirectional cross-attention, and integrates the two modalities using feature-wise gated fusion. We evaluate EVFormer in a single-participant feasibility study using one synchronized public EgoEMG recording with chronologically separated training, validation, and test splits. On 296 test samples, EVFormer achieves a mean absolute error of 11.482 degrees, compared with 13.228-13.610 degrees for vision-only, sEMG-only, late-fusion, and training-mean baselines. This corresponds to relative error reductions of 13.20% compared with the vision-only model and 14.23% compared with late fusion. EVFormer also achieves the lowest error in four of the five evaluated gesture classes. These results provide preliminary evidence that feature-level interaction between egocentric vision and sEMG can improve bimanual hand pose estimation. Further evaluation across participants, recording sessions, sensor placements, and real-world interaction conditions is required to establish the generalizability of the approach.

</details>


### [169] [A Data-Driven Framework for Unsupervised Monitoring of Transmission Systems Using End-of-Line Testing Data: A Case Study at Ford Motor Company](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06980)
*Mohammad N. Bisheh,Mehrdad Moradi,Parinaz Farajiparvar,Colin Brady,Rajesh Gupta,Xueling Li,Javad Navaei,Milad Parvaneh,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种面向高维数据的两阶段多元监控框架，在福特汽车制造真实数据上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感技术产生的高维数据具有复杂非线性模式和强时间依赖性，传统统计监控难以捕捉非线性结构，端线测试中的阈值与启发式规则限制了对高维时间数据中异常特征的检测，而现代深度学习和生成式AI虽预测能力强，但在数据采集昂贵且要求可解释、低延迟、计算高效、非技术人员可用的场景下不适用。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段预处理，去除不完整和无信息样本并对时间序列进行时间对齐；第二阶段进行非线性降维，随后通过基于控制图的Phase I监控过程进行异常检测。框架支持无监督和有监督设置，模块化结构可适配不同领域和运行需求。

Result: 在福特汽车公司汽车制造环境的真实生产数据上评估，所提方法的准确率、召回率和F1分数分别为0.625、1.00和0.769，相比公司现有模型的0.50、0.30和0.429均有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效处理高维时间数据的非线性异常检测，在保持可解释性、低延迟和计算效率的同时，性能优于现有工业模型。

Abstract: Sensing technologies have advanced rapidly across industries ranging from energy to automotive manufacturing. These systems generate high-dimensional (HD) data characterized by complex nonlinear patterns and strong temporal dependencies. Traditional statistical monitoring methods are often limited in their ability to capture such nonlinear structure. Likewise, many analytical approaches used in End-of-Line testing rely on predefined thresholds and heuristic rules, which restrict their ability to detect informative anomaly signatures in HD temporal data. In contrast, while modern deep learning and generative AI models offer strong predictive capabilities, they are often unsuitable in applications where data are costly to collect and where the monitoring system must remain interpretable, low-latency, computationally efficient, and usable by non-technical practitioners. To overcome these limitations, we propose an advanced multivariate monitoring framework for HD data. The framework operates in two stages. In the first stage, the data are preprocessed to remove incomplete and non-informative samples and to temporally align time series data. In the second stage, nonlinear dimensionality reduction is performed, followed by anomaly detection through a control chart based phase I monitoring procedure. The framework can be used in both unsupervised and supervised settings, depending on the availability of ground truth labels during training. Moreover, its flexible and modular structure allows practitioners to adapt its components to different domains and operational requirements. We evaluate the proposed framework on real production data from an automotive manufacturing environment at Ford Motor Company. The proposed method achieves higher accuracy, recall, and F1 score than the company's existing model, improving these metrics from 0.50, 0.30, and 0.429 to 0.625, 1.00, and 0.769, respectively.

</details>


### [170] [An Information-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Benchmark and Model Diagnosis in Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06988)
*Houru Jiang,Zixi Wang,Tengteng Cheng,Xueyi Li,Mingliang Hou,Jiaqi Zheng,Renqiang Luo,Teng Guo,Zitao Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出基于信息论的KT基准诊断框架，利用CTW量化局部可预测性，揭示模型改进在不同熵区间高度不均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KT模型评估主要依赖AUC和准确率等全局聚合指标，掩盖了剩余误差来源，无法判断基准是否接近饱和。

Method: 使用上下文树加权（CTW）对项目-反应历史和当前项目查询建模，构建操作性的因果不确定性坐标，并与完整KT信息集下的不可观测局部不可约不确定性（LIU）区分；将预测投影到该共享不确定性坐标上，按熵带评估模型性能增益。

Result: 在NIPS Task 3/4和Algebra 2005上的综合评估表明，模型改进高度非均匀：现代KT模型在高熵区域获得显著增益，项目感知参考、对数损失和等频率分析支持该局部化结论，并标记出需检查噪声敏感行为的区域。

Conclusion: 该框架通过揭示局部建模失败与真实增益，为研究剩余预测结构及当前KT基准和模型的局限性提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models are predominantly evaluated using aggregate metrics such as area under the curve (AUC) and accuracy. However, these global scores obscure where the remaining errors originate and fail to indicate whether a benchmark is approaching saturation. While estimating a global theoretical performance limit is challenging in realistic KT settings, it is possible to quantify local predictability. To address this, we propose an information-theoretic evaluation framework for KT benchmark diagnosis. We use Context Tree Weighting (CTW) on item-response histories and current-item queries as an operational causal uncertainty coordinate, while distinguishing it from the unobserved Local Irreducible Uncertainty (LIU) under the full KT information set. By projecting predictions onto this shared uncertainty coordinate, we evaluate model performance gains across distinct entropy bands rather than only at the global level. Comprehensive evaluations on NIPS Task 3/4 and Algebra 2005 reveal that model improvements are highly non-uniform. Modern KT models show substantial gains in high-entropy regions, and additional item-aware references, log-loss, and equal-frequency analyses support this localization. The framework also flags regions where apparent gains require checks for noise-sensitive behavior.
