However, real-world legal deployment exhibits substantial case heterogeneity in facts, evidence, and procedural contexts, exposing the limitations of static agent strategies. Moreover, legal reasoning is inherently interdependent across roles and procedural stages, making global reliability fundamentally different from isolated role competence. To address these challenges, we study training-free test-time agent adaptation, where agents continuously exploit deployment-time signals from preceding cases and ongoing interactions without updating model parameters. We propose \method, which introduces \emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} to retrieve reusable experience from previous cases, adapt it to the current factual and procedural context, and consolidate accumulated experience for subsequent decision-making. Further, \emph{Rubric-Aligned Collaboration} verifies and revises role-specific actions according to behavioral and procedural requirements, enabling coordinated decision-making across roles and stages. Extensive experiments on J1-EVAL and LegalWorld across five backbone models demonstrate consistent improvements over representative reasoning and agent baselines with reasonable interaction and computational costs. Ablation and case studies further show that the two components provide complementary benefits in experience adaptation and cross-role coordination, improving the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon legal reasoning.

</details>


### [348] [Towards One-for-All Foundation Model for Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07778)
*Yunhui Liu,Xudong Jin,Kang Zhang,Danshuo An,Yu Xing,Te Song,Jia Liu,Tieke He*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向属性图聚类的通用基础模型OFAG，无需针对每张图训练或调参即可直接应用于多种图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有属性图聚类方法通常为每个图单独训练和调优模型，流程昂贵且脆弱，难以在不同特征空间、结构模式和属性-结构相关性的图之间迁移。

Method: 基于Prior-data Fitted Networks，从广泛的潜在簇、节点属性和图结构先验下生成的合成属性图中学习可复用的聚类推理策略；采用维度无关的信号级图编码器，将每个特征通道视为图信号并建模其对共享图滤波器的响应；使用超球面聚类目标训练，使推理时一次前向传播即可得到聚类友好的节点表示。

Result: 在十个数据集上，一个冻结的OFAG模型在NMI、ACC、ARI和F1上取得最佳平均性能与平均排名，并总计仅用12.43分钟完成全部十个数据集，比第二快基线快6倍以上，在聚类质量上比第二好方法快近28倍。

Conclusion: OFAG可作为属性图聚类的基础模型，无需额外训练或调参即可直接应用于用户自己的属性图数据集，代码与预训练模型已公开。

Abstract: Attributed graph clustering aims to discover node groups by jointly exploiting node attributes and graph topology, yet its unsupervised nature makes model selection and adaptation inherently difficult. Existing methods typically train and tune a separate model for each input graph, leading to costly and fragile pipelines that often fail to transfer across graphs with different feature spaces, structural patterns, and attribute-structure correlations. In this paper, we study a one-for-all alternative: can a single model be trained once and directly applied to diverse attributed graphs without graph-specific training, fine-tuning, or hyperparameter search? We propose OFAG, a foundation model for attributed graph clustering. Building upon Prior-data Fitted Networks, OFAG learns a reusable clustering inference strategy from synthetic attributed graphs generated under broad priors over latent clusters, node attributes, and graph structures. To handle incompatible feature spaces across graphs, OFAG adopts a dimension-agnostic signal-wise graph encoder that treats each feature channel as a graph signal and models its response to shared graph filters. The model is trained with a hyperspherical clustering objective, producing clustering-friendly node representations in a single forward pass at inference time. On ten datasets, one frozen OFAG model achieves the best mean performance and average rank across NMI, ACC, ARI, and F1, while completing all ten datasets in 12.43 minutes total---over 6* faster than the second-fastest baseline and nearly 28* faster than the second-best on clustering quality. Our code and pretrained checkpoint are available at https://github.com/Cloudy1225/OFAG, allowing practitioners to directly apply OFAG to their own attributed graph datasets without additional training or tuning.

</details>


### [349] [ServeLearnBench: How Well Can Agents Self-Improve from Serving Experience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07792)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Shuowei Jin,Beidi Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 论文提出演化环境流式数据集EESD与基准ServeLearnBench，系统评测大模型智能体从服务经验中持续学习的能力，覆盖53个环境窗口、7718项任务及28个模型-学习框架组合，揭示能力与学习之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体在真实环境中部署时，所需行为知识常是隐式、未公开且随时间变化的；现有持续学习评测基准存在部分覆盖问题：或直接给出目标知识，或假设静态环境，或规模与知识多样性有限，因此亟需系统化评测方法。

Method: 形式化演化环境流式数据集EESD，要求智能体从交互与结果反馈中推断、应用并修正隐式环境知识，同时隐藏策略随时间演化；构建ServeLearnBench，涵盖零售支持、银行、销售话术生成三个领域，包含53个环境窗口与7718项任务；评测五种学习框架（RAG、Mem0、SkillOpt、Continual Harness、Prime）与六种模型（GPT-5.6 Terra、Opus 5、Kimi K3、GLM-5.3、DeepSeek V4.1 Flash、GLM-5.3 Flash），共28个模型-框架对、252次学习运行。

Result: 评测得出三项主要发现：任务能力与从经验中学习之间存在显著差距；持续适应代价高昂，且可能破坏原本正确的行为；探索不足是有效适应的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: ServeLearnBench为诊断上述局限提供了受控测试平台，可用于跟踪智能体通过服务经验实现持续、可靠改进的进展。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed to perform complex tasks in real-world environments. However, the knowledge required for correct behavior in these environments is often implicit, undisclosed, and subject to change over time. Recent continual-learning harnesses seek to address this challenge by enabling agents to improve from serving experience. Yet the effectiveness and limitations of these methods are not yet well characterized. Existing benchmarks provide only partial coverage: some explicitly provide the target knowledge, others assume a static environment, and those that support continual adaptation remain limited in scale and knowledge diversity. To enable systematic evaluation, we formalize an evolving-environment streaming dataset (EESD), in which agents must infer, apply, and revise latent environment knowledge from interaction and outcome feedback as hidden policies evolve, and introduce ServeLearnBench, spanning retail support, banking, and sales-pitch generation with 53 environment windows and 7,718 tasks. We evaluate five learning harnesses (RAG, Mem0, SkillOpt, Continual Harness, and Prime) across six models (GPT-5.6 Terra, Opus 5, Kimi K3, GLM-5.3, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, and GLM-5.3 Flash), covering 28 model-harness pairs and 252 learning runs. Our evaluation reveals three main findings: a substantial gap remains between task capability and learning from experience; continual adaptation is costly and can degrade already-correct behavior; and insufficient exploration emerges as a key bottleneck to effective adaptation. Overall, ServeLearnBench provides a controlled testbed for diagnosing these limitations and tracking progress toward agents that continually and reliably improve through serving experience.

</details>


### [350] [Mathematical Proof Assistants for Teaching Logic: The LogiKEy Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08214)
*Christoph Benzmüller,David Fuenmayor,Luca Pasetto*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该文介绍基于LogiKEy方法向计算机科学、数学和哲学混合学生教授逻辑的教学实践，以经典高阶逻辑HOL作为元逻辑嵌入多种对象逻辑，并在单一证明助手（如Isabelle/HOL）中比较与实验逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向不同学科背景的混合学生群体教授逻辑存在困难；同时论证在逻辑课堂中引入证明助手的教学价值，并回应将一切嵌入经典HOL被指为逻辑一元论而非多元论的质疑。

Method: 采用逻辑多元主义的LogiKEy方法，以经典高阶逻辑HOL作为通用元逻辑，通过定义语义对经典与非经典对象逻辑进行语义嵌入；在Isabelle/HOL及其自动定理证明器与（反）模型发现器环境中，设计递进式课堂示例序列：说谎者-诚实者谜题引出命题逻辑到模态逻辑，智者谜题引向动态认知逻辑，Boolos奇特推理展示高阶元逻辑的作用，Chisholm悖论进入道义逻辑并从标准道义逻辑到二元道义逻辑，Gödel本体论论证升至研究级形而上学论证；随后反驳一元论质疑，反思三年教学经验并简述方法在Isabelle之外的移植性。

Result: 形成了一套由前序表示局限、更显式建模需求或新应用驱动的分级课堂示例序列，展示了单一证明助手环境中学习、实验和比较多种逻辑的可行性与教学效果。

Conclusion: LogiKEy式的语义嵌入教学法可有效支持混合背景学生的逻辑教学，其嵌入经典HOL并非逻辑一元论而是逻辑多元主义的实现方式，且该方法具有超越Isabelle的可移植性。

Abstract: We report on an approach to teaching logic to mixed groups of computer science, mathematics, and philosophy students, based on the logico-pluralistic LogiKEy methodology, used for more than a decade in courses, summer schools, and tutorials. LogiKEy uses classical higher-order logic (HOL) as a universal metalogic in which object logics, classical and non-classical alike, are encoded by defining their semantics; through these semantical embeddings a single proof assistant (e.g. Isabelle/HOL), with its automated theorem provers and (counter-)model finders, becomes one environment in which students learn, experiment with, and compare logics. After making the pedagogical case for proof assistants in the logic classroom, we present a graded sequence of classroom examples, each transition motivated by a limitation of the preceding representation, by a need for more explicit modelling resources, or by a new application. A liars-and-truth-tellers puzzle leads from propositional to modal logic; the Wise Men puzzle leads on to dynamic epistemic logic; Boolos's curious inference illustrates what a higher-order meta-logic buys, even for automated proof search; Chisholm's paradox takes the sequence into deontic logic, and from standard to dyadic deontic logic; and Gödel's ontological argument brings it to a research-level metaphysical argument. We then rebut the objection that embedding everything in classical HOL is monism rather than pluralism, reflect on three years of teaching such a course, and sketch the portability of the approach beyond Isabelle.

</details>


### [351] [Adaptive Mean Estimation by In-Context Learning: A Gradient-Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07804)
*Martin Eppert,Krishna Balasubramanian,Subhro Ghosh,Jason Klusowski,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上分析了先验拟合网络（PFNs）在位置估计任务中学习统计自适应性的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFNs（如TabPFN）可媲美传统统计方法，但其统计自适应性如何学习尚不明确，需在受控问题中研究。

Method: 在位置估计中，隐藏数据族（高斯、均匀、对称高斯混合），用softmax注意力计算经验累积生成函数导数作为特征，结合softmax专家混合或GLU，并分析逐阶段梯度流。

Result: 在约Ω(n^{1+ε})预训练任务下，学习估计器在高斯任务上渐近有效，均匀任务上达极小极大率n^ε倍，混合任务在收缩方差机制下序最优；风险分解为专家误差、路由误差和归一化误差；softmax门控强制归一化和平移等变性，而GLU需学习，其动态分快速偏置消除与慢速专家选择两阶段。

Conclusion: 端到端实验证实了预测的专家特化，并阐明了softmax与GLU架构差异带来的自适应学习机制。

Abstract: Prior Fitted Networks (PFNs) such as TabPFN now rival established statistical procedures across prediction and estimation tasks. A natural explanation is that PFNs have the property of statistical adaptivity, that is, they perform nearly as well as a method tailored to the true data-generating model for a heterogeneous set of models, while not being told which model the data comes from. We study how such adaptivity is learned in a controlled location-estimation problem. Each task is an unlabeled sample whose family is hidden: Gaussian data call for averaging, with error of order $n^{-1}$, whereas uniform data are best estimated from their extremes, at the faster rate $n^{-2}$. We also provide the example of a symmetric Gaussian mixture, for which a rate of $σ^2_n/n$ can be attained. On scalar inputs, softmax attention computes the derivative of the empirical cumulant-generating function. A single primitive therefore both supplies features that distinguish the families and forms estimators interpolating between the sample mean and the mid-range. We combine attention experts through either a softmax mixture of experts or a gated linear unit (GLU), and analyze stagewise gradient flow. With $\widetildeΩ(n^{1+ε})$ pretraining tasks, the learned estimator is asymptotically efficient on Gaussian tasks, within a factor $n^ε$ of the minimax rate on uniform tasks, and order-optimal on mixtures in a shrinking-variance regime. These guarantees extend to new locations and longer contexts. A risk decomposition separates expert error, routing error and normalization error, which clarifies the architectural contrast. Softmax gating enforces normalization and exact translation equivariance, whereas the GLU must learn it: its dynamics separate into fast bias removal followed by slow expert selection. End-to-end experiments recover the predicted specialization.

</details>


### [352] [Learn2Play Bench: How Well Do LLM Agents Learn from Experience in Unfamiliar Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08215)
*Yibo Li,Jinhang Qiu,Zhi Zheng,Qianyun Guo,Jiaying Wu,Shuo Ji,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Learn2Play Bench基准，用规则新颖的文本游戏评估LLM智能体从交互中学习的能力，并发现经验保留、与人类的差距及框架设计三个关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多基于指令中已知或预训练熟悉的规则，难以区分智能体是通过交互学习还是仅凭已有知识推理，因此无法准确评估其在新环境中的学习能力。

Method: 设计规则新颖或反直觉的文本游戏，提供可复现反馈与自动评分，支持跨重复尝试的受控评估；通过改变游戏实例测试知识迁移；系统评估骨干模型、自演化方法和智能体框架对学习能力的影响。

Result: （1）经验保留：保留完整的动作与反馈记录比总结为规则或策略更有效；（2）人机差距：顶尖人类玩家峰值分数更高，探索策略更多样且重复动作更少；（3）框架重要性：固定骨干模型时，更换框架可提升性能并降低估算推理成本。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLM智能体从经验中学习的机制，并为未来提升其学习能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for LLM agents to adapt to unfamiliar and dynmaic environments. Evaluating this ability is therefore important for understanding how effectively agents acquire and use new knowledge. Existing benchmarks have sought to evaluate this ability, but they primarily evaluate tasks whose rules are provided in the instructions or already familiar to pretrained models, making it difficult to distinguish learning from interactions from reasoning with existing knowledge. To address this, we introduce Learn2Play Bench, a benchmark of newly designed text-based games, whose rules are novel or counterintuitive, requiring agents to acquire knowledge through interaction rather than rely solely on pretrained knowledge. These games provide reproducible feedback and automatic scoring, enabling controlled evaluation of learning across repeated attempts. We also vary game instances to test whether agents can apply what they have learned to new situations. Therefore, we evaluate how backbone models, self-evolving methods, and agent harnesses affect agents' learning ability, revealing three findings: (1) Experience retention: Retaining complete records of actions and feedback can support more effective learning than summarizing these experiences into rules or strategies. (2) Human agent gap: Top-performing human players achieve higher peak scores than the evaluated agents. Human explore more varied strategies, and repeat actions less. (3) Harness matters: With the backbone fixed, changing the harness can improve performance while reducing estimated inference cost. Together, these findings provide insights into how LLM agents learn from experience and suggest directions for future work to improve their learning ability. Project website: https://liushiliushi.github.io/learn2play-bench-website/

</details>


### [353] [SIFT: Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer for Multi-Task Personalized Filter Ranking at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07810)
*Shashank Dabriwal,Tanya Piplani,Hao Li,Yiwei Wang,Ashish Jain,Kedar Bellare,Stephanie Moyerman*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SIFT，基于Transformer从原始行为序列学习用户偏好，替代手工特征，统一支持多种搜索过滤器排序，并在离线与在线实验中显著提升预订和过滤器使用指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产过滤器排序系统依赖ETL生成的手工、预聚合特征，维护成本高且难以扩展至新过滤器类型或上下文维度（如行程长度、团体规模）。

Method: SIFT（Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer）直接建模原始行为序列，生成统一用户表示，并接入多个预测任务：预订可能性、过滤器参与度、序数容量阈值（如2+卧室）。支持布尔和数值范围过滤器，扩展新过滤器仅需添加新预测头。用户表示离线按日计算以保证服务速度。

Result: 离线：预订和设施参与度PR-AUC分别比生产基线提升51.9%和62.8%。在线A/B：推荐过滤器参与度+20.0%，搜索者整体过滤器使用+0.72%，新支持的卧室、浴室、床过滤器使用分别+3.9%、+10.7%、+0.52%。快速集成新酒店意图过滤器后，未取消酒店预订+3.8%，整体市场预订+0.76%。

Conclusion: SIFT已全面部署生产，为百万级用户提供可扩展的个性化服务，验证了统一用户表示框架在双边市场过滤器排序中的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Search filters help guests navigate vast catalogs in two-sided marketplaces like Airbnb, and recommending the right filters can meaningfully lift booking conversion. Many such production filter-ranking systems, however, represent the guest through hand-engineered, pre-aggregated features generated by ETL pipelines. This makes it expensive to maintain and difficult to extend for new filter types or contextual dimensions (trip length, group size). We present SIFT (Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer), a ranking model built on transformers that learns guest preferences directly from raw behavioral sequences. SIFT replaces manual feature engineering with a unified guest representation that feeds multiple prediction tasks, including booking likelihood, filter engagement, and ordinal capacity thresholds (e.g., 2+ bedrooms) -- a general framework for filter ranking in two-sided marketplaces that accommodates both boolean and numeric-range filter types. Extending SIFT to new filters requires only adding a new head, not a new feature pipeline. To keep serving fast, this guest representation is computed offline on a daily cadence rather than at request time. Offline, SIFT improves booking and amenity-engagement PR-AUC by +51.9% and +62.8% respectively over the production baseline. In online A/B testing, SIFT increased engagement with recommended filters by +20.0%, overall filter usage among searchers by +0.72%, and usage of the newly-supported bedroom, bathroom, and bed filters by +3.9%, +10.7%, and +0.52% respectively. Demonstrating the system's extensibility, we rapidly integrated a novel hotel-intent filter using the same shared representation, driving a +3.8% lift in uncancelled hotel bookings and a +0.76% lift in overall marketplace bookings. SIFT is now fully deployed in production, serving scalable personalization to millions of guests.

</details>


### [354] [Confidence-Ordering Reversal under Contextual Priors in Neural Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08229)
*Xinyu Zhang,Sichao Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 上下文先验通过重塑候选得分提升神经到语言解码，但混淆了置信度估计，导致初始错误预测出现置信度顺序反转：正确候选初始排名不同，融合后的top-two margin对修复与残留错误的判别能力相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法用融合后的得分计算置信度，但先验在修正错误的同时会改变得分分布，使残留错误获得更高置信度而准确率不变，因此需要研究先验如何影响置信度并改进选择性解码。

Method: 在MEG-MASC和MOUS数据集上，使用局部解码得分，通过加性浅融合结合上下文先验，以融合后的top-two margin作为置信度；分析不同初始排名下修复与残留错误的AUROC，并进行仅改变融合权重的因果干预。

Result: 初始错误预测中，正确候选初始排名2-3时修复AUROC为0.70，排名21-50时降至0.39；排名20以后的错误占融合后总错误的46.6%。因果干预改变融合权重可将反转移至更深排名，且在准确率峰值后仍移动。分开读取局部与先验得分后，解码器响应窗口比例从56.7%提升至74.5%，输出集包含正确候选的比例保持92%。

Conclusion: 上下文融合后的置信度应保留局部和上下文证据，而非仅使用融合得分；分开读取两类得分可改善选择性解码。

Abstract: Contextual priors improve neural-to-language decoding by reshaping candidate scores. However, confidence is read from the same reshaped scores, so the errors a prior leaves behind can become more confident with no change in accuracy to reveal it. We study how a prior shapes confidence in speech retrieval on MEG-MASC and MOUS using local decoding scores, a contextual prior combined by additive shallow fusion, and the fused top-two margin as confidence. Among initially incorrect predictions, we find a confidence-ordering reversal: a larger margin makes a repair more likely when the correct candidate starts near the top of the local ranking, but less likely when it starts lower. On MEG-MASC, pooled correctness AUROC is 0.87, yet AUROC separating repairs from residual errors falls from 0.70 at initial ranks 2-3 to 0.39 at ranks 21-50. Errors starting beyond rank 20, inside the reversed region, make up 46.6% of all post-fusion errors. We propose a score-level account: a repair must first close the correct candidate's initial deficit, limiting its final margin, whereas a residual error can build a large margin between two incorrect candidates. A causal intervention that changes only the fusion weight moves the reversal to deeper ranks as predicted. Under a word-level LM prior, it keeps moving after accuracy gain peaks, so a weight chosen for accuracy does not settle confidence. Reading local and prior scores separately improves selective decoding: the decoder answers on 74.5% of windows instead of 56.7%, while 92% of output sets still contain the correct candidate. Confidence after contextual fusion should retain the local and contextual evidence behind each prediction, not just the fused scores. Project website: https://confidencereversal.github.io/; Code: https://github.com/AmadeusFake/NeuDecodingConfReversal

</details>


### [355] [$α$Transfer: Coefficient Transfer for Efficient Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07819)
*Shih-Cheng Huang,Zhi Rui Tam,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Hung-yi Lee,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出αTransfer范式，利用同家族模型合并系数性能分布高度相似的特性，在小代理模型上搜索最优合并系数并直接迁移到大模型，显著降低搜索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并需搜索最优合并系数，但随模型规模和数量增长，搜索因高内存需求和组合爆炸而代价过高。

Method: 发现同模型家族内不同规模模型的合并系数性能分布高度一致，据此在小代理模型上搜索最优合并系数，再迁移到大型目标模型；在多种合并方法、模型家族和任务上验证。

Result: 在视觉Transformer上实现6倍加速和70%内存降低，在大语言模型上实现20倍加速和85%内存降低，同时保持可比性能。

Conclusion: αTransfer是一种高效且可泛化的模型合并扩展方法，为降低合并系数搜索成本提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Model merging offers a promising solution for combining multiple fine-tuned checkpoints into a single model through parameter arithmetic. However, finding optimal merging coefficients requires an extensive search that becomes prohibitively expensive as models scale in both size and number, due to high memory requirements and combinatorial growth in the search space. We show that, within the same model family, models exhibit highly congruent performance distributions over merging coefficients across different model sizes. This distributional similarity enables a practical paradigm we call \textit{$α$Transfer}: searching for optimal coefficients on a small proxy model, then directly transfer them to larger target models. We verify $α$Transfer across multiple merging methods, model families, and tasks. Experimental results demonstrate a 6$\times$ speedup and 70\% memory reduction on vision transformers, and a 20$\times$ speedup and 85\% memory reduction on large language models, while maintaining comparable performance. Our findings establish $α$Transfer as an efficient and generalizable approach to scaling model merging.

</details>


### [356] [OSFP4: Joint Optimization of Diagonal Smoothing and Block Scales for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08231)
*Neriah Ben David,Ori Meir,Or Ordentlich*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出OSFP4量化方案，通过优化对角平滑矩阵与块缩放，降低NVFP4下LLM线性投影的矩阵乘积量化误差，在保持接近原生吞吐的同时取得最优平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4虽存储紧凑且有张量核加速，但精度保持需精细量化，现有方案在量化误差控制上仍有不足。

Method: 针对每个线性投影引入对角平滑矩阵，在NVFP4下（考虑round-to-nearest或GPTQ式逐次干扰消除的舍入过程）联合优化平滑项与块缩放，通过分析乘性抖动FP4量化器实现联合优化。

Result: 在对应量化设置下，OSFP4在评估竞争者中取得最高平均精度，并在所测负载上保留约94-97%的厂商NVFP4预填充吞吐。

Conclusion: OSFP4是一种兼顾精度与效率的NVFP4量化方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an attractive datatype for large language model (LLM) inference, offering compact storage and native tensor-core acceleration. However, preserving accuracy using NVFP4 requires careful quantization. In this work we develop a novel quantization scheme called Optimized Smoothing and Scaling for NVFP4 (OSFP4). For each linear projection it uses a diagonal smoothing matrix whose entries are optimized to minimize the squared matrix-product quantization error under NVFP4, taking into account the rounding procedure that is used (either round-to-nearest, or GPTQ-style successive interference cancellation). This requires performing joint optimization on the smoothing entries as well as the block scales, which is facilitated by analyzing a multiplicative-dither FP4 quantizer instead of the fixed deterministic one. Experiments show that OSFP4 achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated competitors in the corresponding quantization settings, while retaining approximately 94-97\% of vendor NVFP4 prefill throughput on the measured workloads. Our code is available in https://github.com/neriahbd/OSFP4

</details>


### [357] [CANDLE: Cortical Null-Space Decomposition for Noninvasive Brain Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07824)
*Shuntaro Suzuki,Yuiga Wada,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CANDLE，一种基于学习的电生理源成像模型，在个体化皮层几何上估计源活动，通过零空间先验学习提升泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电生理源成像（ESI）本质上是病态的，源活动维度远高于传感器观测，导致解不唯一。现有学习方法虽引入数据驱动源先验，但难以泛化到个体化皮层几何。

Method: CANDLE学习由T1加权MRI得到的源到传感器映射所诱导的零空间上的先验，将学习限制在不可观测的源成分，同时保持几何约束。训练使用全脑模拟器，涵盖超过1100个个体化皮层几何和源自26000多个统计脑图的源配置。

Result: 仅在模拟数据上训练，CANDLE在模拟源活动估计上优于先前ESI方法，并泛化到两个实证任务：同时记录的头皮EEG的颅内刺激定位，以及术前发作间期EEG的致痫区估计。

Conclusion: CANDLE通过零空间先验学习有效解决个体化皮层几何泛化问题，在模拟和实证任务中均表现优越，项目页面见https://candle-esi.pages.dev。

Abstract: Electrophysiological source imaging (ESI) aims to estimate cortical source activity from noninvasive electrophysiological measurements such as electroencephalogram (EEG). However, ESI is fundamentally ill-posed because source activity is substantially higher-dimensional than sensor observations, resulting in non-unique solutions. Recent learning-based approaches address this ambiguity by learning data-driven source priors, yet they often struggle to generalize across subject-specific cortical geometries. To address this, we propose CANDLE, a learning-based ESI model that estimates source activity on subject-specific cortical geometries. CANDLE learns a prior over the null space induced by the source-to-sensor mapping derived from T1-weighted MRI, restricting learning to unobservable source components while preserving geometric constraints. To train CANDLE, we develop a whole-brain simulator spanning over 1,100 subject-specific cortical geometries with source configurations derived from over 26,000 statistical brain maps. Trained exclusively on simulated data, CANDLE outperformed prior ESI methods on simulated source activity estimation and generalized to two empirical tasks: (i) intracranial stimulation localization from simultaneously recorded scalp EEG and (ii) epileptogenic zone estimation from presurgical interictal EEG. Our project page is available at https://candle-esi.pages.dev}{https://candle-esi.pages.dev.

</details>


### [358] [LeanPlan: Optimal Planning with LLM-Generated Heuristics and Admissibility Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08246)
*André G. Pereira,Augusto B. Corrêa,Felipe Meneguzzi,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LeanPlan，首个利用LLM生成可机器验证可采纳性启发式的最优规划系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的启发式虽在满足性规划中表现优异，但无法保证可采纳性，可能导致次优解。

Method: 采用智能体循环，借助规划器反馈迭代改进可复用的领域特定启发式及其可采纳性证明和领域假设，并在Lean 4中实现启发式、证明及高效规划器（含机器验证的实例化与搜索）。

Result: 在IPC十个领域及三个新领域上评估，测试任务对象数最多为训练任务的57倍；使用GPT-5.6 Sol可成功为所有领域生成启发式与可采纳性证明；相比最先进的Scorpion规划器通常扩展更少状态，总体求解更多任务。

Conclusion: LeanPlan实现了LLM生成可采纳启发式的最优规划，并保证其正确性，在扩展性和求解能力上优于现有最优规划器。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) can generate heuristic functions that guide search to achieve state-of-the-art performance in satisficing planning, where any plan is acceptable. However, these heuristics are not guaranteed to be admissible and can lead to suboptimal plans. We introduce LeanPlan, the first planning system that finds optimal plans with LLM-generated heuristics whose admissibility is machine-checked. Given a domain description and training tasks, an agentic loop uses planner feedback to iteratively improve a reusable domain-specific heuristic, its admissibility proof and the required domain assumptions. LeanPlan implements the heuristic, its proof and an efficient planner with machine-checked grounding and search in Lean 4. We evaluate LeanPlan on ten domains from the International Planning Competition and three new domains, using test tasks with up to 57 times as many objects as the training tasks. With GPT-5.6 Sol in the agentic loop, we successfully generate heuristics and admissibility proofs for all these domains. With the resulting heuristics, LeanPlan usually expands fewer states than the state-of-the-art Scorpion planner and solves more tasks overall.

</details>


### [359] [Retrieval Is Not Enough: Refreshing Memory for Frozen Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07834)
*Chao He,Jianyu Xu,Xinyi Guo,Ruiqi Liu,Haobin Ding,Ruiqi He,Dongqing Song*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FreshCast，一种即插即用的检索增强时间序列预测框架，通过持续更新非参数记忆并闭式校准权重，提升冻结预测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强方法在训练阶段构建一次记忆，部署后新观测无法利用，且未校准检索信息对冻结预测器的影响权重；检索效用取决于历史是否仍反映当前状态及校正是否与预测器残差对齐，而该对齐在验证与部署间可能因记忆陈旧而漂移。

Method: FreshCast保持预测器冻结，用新观测持续更新非参数记忆，通过关系核回归形成记忆预测，并在验证段以闭式解校准其权重；在简化生成模型下，用预测器误差与记忆校正的二阶关系刻画最优组合增益，并证明足够长的回看窗口可使周期性记忆信息冗余。

Result: 在七个基准和十种预测架构上，FreshCast对每个预测器和输入长度均降低平均MSE，输入长度96和720时分别降低14.6%和5.6%，并在比较设置下MSE低于所评估的检索增强和在线基线；消融显示训练结束时冻结记忆会消除大部分增益。

Conclusion: 对冻结预测器而言，有用的历史参考必须保持时效性，并提供有助于校正其剩余误差的信息；训练后观测是性能提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented time-series forecasting uses the continuations of historical segments similar to the current context as references for a forecaster. Most existing methods build the retrieval memory once from the training segment, leaving observations revealed after deployment unavailable as references, and generally do not calibrate how much the retrieved information should influence a frozen forecaster. We identify two key determinants of retrieval utility for a frozen forecaster: whether the history still reflects the current state, and whether the correction it induces aligns with the forecaster's residual errors, an alignment that can shift between validation and deployment when the memory becomes stale. We propose FreshCast, a plug-in retrieval framework that keeps the forecaster frozen, continuously updates a non-parametric memory with new observations, forms a memory forecast through relational kernel regression, and calibrates its weight in closed form on the validation segment. Under a simplified generative model, we characterize the optimal combination gain through the second-order relation between forecaster error and memory correction, and show that a sufficiently long look-back can make periodic memory information redundant. Across seven benchmarks and ten forecasting architectures, FreshCast reduces average MSE for every evaluated forecaster and input length, by 14.6% and 5.6% at input lengths 96 and 720, and achieves lower MSE than the evaluated retrieval-augmented and online baselines in their comparison settings. Ablations show that freezing the memory at the end of training removes most of the gain, identifying post-training observations as a primary source of improvement. For a frozen forecaster, useful historical references must remain timely and provide information that helps correct its remaining errors.

</details>


### [360] [MASC: A Multi-Agent Self-Calibration Framework with Latent Construct Alignment for Consistent Client Role-Playing in Psychological Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08250)
*Shixin Peng,Kun Jiang,Jiaxing Zheng,Qihao Yang,Jingying Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MASC多智能体自校准框架，通过潜在构念对齐提升心理咨询中来访者角色扮演的心理一致性，并构建CRPC-Bench基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色扮演方法依赖静态档案提示，易导致人设漂移、不现实配合或心理状态、沟通行为与情绪不一致；同时缺乏同时评估稳定来访者特征与动态心理变化的统一测试平台。

Method: MASC结合构念引导生成、协作精炼、一致性验证和基于记忆的修订，形成闭环校准循环，在对话过程中检测并纠正不一致；同时构建CRPC-Bench基准，涵盖会话级档案信息与大五人格特质，以及轮次级心理状态、沟通行为和情绪表达，包含38个动机式访谈来访者档案及人格与情绪标注。

Result: 实验表明，MASC在档案、人格、接受度和轮次级一致性上均优于现有方法，其中异构配置取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: MASC与CRPC-Bench为AI辅助心理咨询研究与训练中开发及评估心理一致的来访者模拟提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate clients for counselor training and psychological counseling research, but reliable simulation requires clients to remain psychologically coherent across extended interactions. Existing role-playing methods largely rely on static profile prompts and may exhibit persona drift, unrealistic cooperativeness, or inconsistent psychological states, communicative actions, and emotions. Existing evaluations also lack a unified testbed for both stable client characteristics and evolving psychological dynamics. We propose MASC, a Multi-Agent Self-Calibration framework with latent construct alignment for consistent client role-playing in psychological counseling. MASC combines construct-guided generation, collaborative refinement, consistency verification, and memory-based revision in a closed calibration loop that detects and corrects inconsistencies as dialogue unfolds. We further introduce CRPC-Bench, a benchmark covering session-level profile information and Big-Five personality traits, as well as turn-level psychological state, communicative action, and emotion expression. CRPC-Bench contains 38 motivational interviewing client profiles augmented with personality and emotion annotations. Experiments show that MASC outperforms existing methods across profile, personality, receptivity, and turn-level consistency, with the heterogeneous configuration achieving the strongest overall performance. MASC and CRPC-Bench provide a unified foundation for developing and evaluating psychologically coherent client simulations for AI-assisted counseling research and training.

</details>


### [361] [The Standardization Trap: Certifying Joint Label Processing in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08314)
*Duong Nguyen,Nicolas Chesneau,Milan Bhan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究检验预训练表格基础模型（TFMs）是否采用固定权重映射进行上下文学习，提出两种仅依赖标准化标签预测的证书，发现五个公开TFM均存在联合处理现象，且该现象随训练出现并主要由注意力分数承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性回归与核平滑均用固定权重解释上下文学习：特征决定每个上下文标签的权重。但预训练表格基础模型是否也遵循固定权重解释尚不清楚。直接用导数检验会遇到标准化陷阱：公开TFM包会在模型看到标签前标准化标签，而普通导数还反映标准化标签集之外的行为，可能把与固定权重映射完全一致的模型误判为非线性。

Method: 提出两种证书，仅依赖模型在标准化标签上的预测，可分别拒绝两种解释：固定权重预测，以及独立非线性标签变换之和。在五个公开TFM上评估这些证书。

Result: 五个公开TFM均未通过证书：改变一个上下文标签会改变其他标签对预测的影响，作者称之为联合处理。进一步发现联合处理随训练而出现，且注意力分数承载了大部分测得交互。

Conclusion: 这些发现要求对TFM的解释必须考虑上下文标签如何改变单个样例的影响力，即需要能刻画联合处理的解释框架。

Abstract: Linear regression and kernel smoothing offer tractable explanations of in-context learning: in both, the features determine the weight assigned to each context label. However, whether this fixed-weight account describes pretrained tabular foundation models (TFMs) remains unclear. Testing this account using derivatives runs into a standardization trap: public TFM packages standardize the labels before the model sees them, yet ordinary derivatives also reflect behavior outside the set of standardized labels, making a model appear nonlinear even when every prediction it makes agrees with a fixed-weight map. We propose two certificates that depend only on predictions at standardized labels and can reject two distinct explanations: fixed-weight prediction and sums of independent nonlinear label transformations. Across the five public TFMs that we evaluate, our certificates show that changing one context label alters how other labels influence the prediction, a behavior we call joint processing. We further find that joint processing emerges with training and that attention scores carry most of the measured interaction. Together, these findings motivate TFM explanations that account for how context labels change the influence of individual examples.

</details>


### [362] [SCOPE: Certified Theorem Proving with a Language Model as the Policy Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08319)
*Hanchao Zhou,Jialei Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE方法，将证明生成分解为算子规划、符号执行与编译器渲染，在Lean证明助手中显著提升多步数值命题的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接生成证明在多步数值命题上受限于每个整数正确率的k次方，导致通过率极低。

Method: SCOPE（状态条件算子规划与执行）让模型在算子词汇表上规划，符号引擎执行数值计算，编译器渲染证明；使用当前引擎状态而非滞后状态作为决策帧。

Result: 在218题套件上，135M骨干模型认证191/218（87.6%），而7B DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5-RL仅认证18/218且消耗更多资源；Lean-Workbook上认证2132/3536（60.29%），主套件无退化。

Conclusion: 限制自由生成并保持决策时信息可见，比扩大模型规模更直接有效。

Abstract: In proof assistants such as Lean, a generated proof must pass machine compilation checks, so evaluation needs no human scoring. Direct generation fails on multi-step numeric propositions: a proof is valid only if every content integer is correct, so the pass rate is bounded by the k-th power of the per-integer accuracy. Controlled corruption across 2,617 reference proofs confirms this power law. SCOPE (State-Conditioned Operator Planning and Execution) enforces the natural division of labor: the model plans over an operator vocabulary, a symbolic engine executes the numerics, and a compiler renders the proof. On a 218-problem suite it certifies 191/218 (87.6%) with a 135M backbone; the 7B DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5-RL certifies 18/218 at 27.5 times the tokens and 37.5 times the wall-clock, and DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B certifies zero on a bidirectional dual suite. Multi-step thinking costs 6.12 discrete decision actions per problem and produces no natural-language thinking text. Replacing the lagged engine state in the decision frame with the current one lifts the pass rate from 117/218 to 191/218, while up-weighting the chain-end loss hurts. On the public Lean-Workbook library, 2,132 of 3,536 gradeable admissible problems certify (60.29%) with zero regression on the main suite. All readings come from a version-frozen review with independent rechecks and reverse verification. Restricting free generation and keeping decision-time information visible is a more direct route than enlarging the model.

</details>


### [363] [On-Policy Distillation with Negative-Policy Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07874)
*Jaehui Hwang,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun,Byeongho Heo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NP-OPD，在on-policy蒸馏的rollout阶段引入能力较弱的负策略，用其偏好token提供负向信号，弥补强教师与学生分布重叠不足导致的监督不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD仅依赖教师的正向指导，当教师与学生分布重叠有限时，正向监督信号不足，学生难以获得有效学习信号。

Method: 不改变蒸馏奖励形式，而是在rollout阶段持续引入低性能负策略生成的token，使负策略偏好高于教师的token持续暴露于教师监督下，从而在保留正向教师监督的同时提供显式负向信号。

Result: 在多种模型规模、生成模式、推理领域及不同OPD变体上，NP-OPD均优于原始OPD；分析显示其能有效抑制负策略相对教师偏好的token，并使学生远离负策略。

Conclusion: 通过负策略rollout引入负向信号的设计有效，并揭示了rollout策略在OPD中的新作用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has been widely studied as a post-training method in which a student model obtains token-level supervision from a stronger teacher on its own rollouts. Recent studies have improved OPD through alternative distillation reward formulations and teacher configurations, while the objective of distillation remains centered on mimicking the teacher. However, when a stronger teacher has limited distributional overlap with the student, such positive guidance can provide insufficient learning signals. In this work, we introduce Negative-Policy OPD (NP-OPD), which complements teacher supervision with rollouts from a lower-performing, lower-capability negative policy that serves as a negative reference for the student. Rather than modifying the distillation reward formulation, NP-OPD introduces the negative policy at the rollout stage, continuously supplying tokens preferred by the negative policy over the teacher so that they remain exposed to teacher supervision throughout training. This provides an explicit negative signal through negative-policy rollouts while preserving the positive teacher supervision used in OPD. Through extensive experiments, we show that NP-OPD improves OPD across model scales, generation modes, reasoning domains, and different OPD variants. Furthermore, our analyses show that NP-OPD effectively suppresses tokens preferred by the negative policy over the teacher and moves the student away from the negative policy. These results support our design of introducing negative signals through negative-policy rollouts and provide new insight into the role of the rollout policy in OPD. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/np-opd.

</details>


### [364] [MedZERO: Self-Evolving Agents for Open-Ended Medical Reasoning Through Controlled Knowledge Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08327)
*Xilin Dang,Weilin Ruan,Xue Yang,Jinghao Wang,Xiaowei Hu,Jinpeng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MedZERO自进化框架，通过Examiner生成前沿医学问题与Reasoner结合外部知识工具的多轮推理，实现开放式医学推理的持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法依赖精确答案或可执行程序验证，难以适用于开放式、知识密集且仅部分可验证的医学推理领域。

Method: MedZERO由Examiner（生成前沿医学问题-选项对）和Reasoner（基于证据的多轮推理，调用外部知识工具）组成，并采用受控知识积累机制，维护临时探索性知识与精选持久性知识。

Result: 在五个公开医学推理基准上，使用4B和8B规模基础模型进行开放式评估，MedZERO一致优于基础模型及先前自进化基线，最高平均准确率提升达13.7个百分点。

Conclusion: MedZERO能有效突破静态参数知识与昂贵专家监督的限制，为开放式医学推理提供可靠且持续的自进化改进路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in medical question answering and clinical reasoning, yet their improvement remains constrained by static parametric knowledge and costly expert supervision. Self-evolving agents offer a promising alternative by enabling models to improve through iterative task generation and problem-solving. However, most existing self-evolving methods are designed for easily verifiable domains such as mathematics and coding, where solutions can be checked by exact answers or executable programs. Medical reasoning is fundamentally different: it is open-ended, knowledge-intensive, and often only partially verifiable. We present MedZERO, a self-evolving framework for open-ended medical reasoning. MedZERO couples an Examiner that generates frontier medical question-option pairs with a Reasoner that solves them through evidence-grounded multi-turn reasoning with external knowledge tools. To support reliable, continual improvement, MedZERO adopts controlled knowledge accumulation, which maintains temporary exploratory knowledge and curated persistent knowledge in reasoning. We evaluate MedZERO on five public medical reasoning benchmarks using 4B- and 8B-scale base models under open-ended evaluation. Across all settings, MedZERO consistently outperforms the underlying base models and prior self-evolving baselines, achieving up to 13.7 average accuracy-point gains over the next-best self-evolving baseline.

</details>


### [365] [ApexQuant: Data-Free Elastic Quantization by Residual Re-Isotropization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07904)
*Aksel Fristrup,Sumit Pandey,Ankit Kariryaa*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出免校准量化方法 ApexQuant，通过递归重量化残差误差作为现有量化器的精炼层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法通常需要校准数据，而在医学影像和地球观测等领域，因许可限制或隐私约束往往无法获得分布内数据，因此需要一种完全无数据的量化方案。

Method: 基于新鲜随机旋转使每个残差回到超球面上均匀分布的性质，刻画逐次传递中渐进误差衰减速率；在读取任何权重前即可确定某层达到目标权重空间误差所需的传递次数；每次前缀本身即为有效低率模型，一个产物可服务多种精度；实例化三个可互换阶段：标量、E8 与网格（trellis）。

Result: 在四个开放权重 LLM 以及地球观测和医学领域上验证；渐进重各向同性化在四比特下接近全精度仅差几个百分点，并在完全无数据设置下取得了所测最佳二比特方案。

Conclusion: ApexQuant 在无需校准数据的情况下实现了接近全精度的低比特量化，并支持单一模型产物服务多种精度。

Abstract: We introduce ApexQuant, a calibration-free quantization method that recursively re-quantizes the residual error, serving as a refinement layer on top of existing quantizers. We establish that a fresh random rotation returns each residual to the uniform distribution on the hypersphere, which characterizes the rate of progressive error decay across successive passes. This result lets us determine, before any weight is read, how many passes a layer needs for a target weight-space error. Every prefix is itself a valid lower-rate model, so one artifact serves several precisions. We instantiate ApexQuant with three interchangeable stages, scalar, $E_8$ and trellis, and validate it on four open-weight LLMs and on Earth-observation and medical domains where in-distribution data is often unattainable as imagery arrives under restrictive licences or due to patient material under privacy constraints. Progressive re-isotropization comes within a few percent of full precision at four bits and gives the best two-bit arm we measure, in a completely data-free setting.

</details>


### [366] [Revisiting Temporal Regularization for Smooth Control in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07910)
*SungJae Ahn,Jeong Woon Lee,Kyoleen Kwak,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文证明时间平滑惩罚可隐含地提供空间平滑性，并提出仅用时间平滑性的条件化方法CATS，以在不牺牲任务性能的前提下减少动作振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略产生的非平滑动作振荡会阻碍其在物理机器人上的部署。现有空间平滑方法（架构或惩罚）在追求更强平滑时会降低任务性能；而时间正则化虽约束沿转移的动作差异，但被认为在观测噪声下无法提供所需的空间平滑性。

Method: 作者重新审视上述假设，证明时间惩罚能够约束当前状态（共享同一后继状态）之间的期望动作差异，从而揭示其空间效应并经验性地扩展到空间平滑。基于此，提出CATS：仅使用时间平滑性进行动作条件化，将时间惩罚与线性递增（linear ramp-up）相结合，使策略先学习获得奖励的行为，再逐步平滑其动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，CATS显著减少了动作振荡，同时未降低任务性能，且计算开销很小。

Conclusion: 时间正则化能够在更好保留任务性能的同时提供空间平滑性，优于显式的空间正则化；CATS通过线性递增策略兼顾了回报保持与时间、空间平滑性。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning policies can produce nonsmooth action oscillations that hinder deployment on physical robots. Existing architectural and penalty-based approaches seek spatial smoothness by directly reducing sensitivity to changes in state inputs, but their broad constraints can degrade task performance as stronger smoothing is pursued. Temporal regularization instead constrains action differences along observed transitions, but has been considered unable to provide the spatial smoothness needed under observation noise. We revisit this assumption by proving that the temporal penalty bounds the expected action differences between current states sharing a next state, revealing a spatial effect that empirically extends to spatial smoothness. Building on this finding, we propose Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness (CATS), which combines a temporal penalty with linear ramp-up. We highlight temporal regularization's ability to provide spatial smoothness while better preserving task performance than explicit spatial regularization. Through linear ramp-up, CATS allows the policy to learn rewarding behavior before progressively smoothing its actions, improving return preservation and both temporal and spatial smoothness. Experiments in both simulation and the real world show that CATS substantially reduces action oscillation without degrading task performance, with little computational overhead.

</details>


### [367] [EMHO: EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization via Experience Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08432)
*Hyun Jung Lee,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Tae-Eui Kam,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EMHO框架，在冻结具身模型的前提下，通过分析执行轨迹与历史来迭代优化智能体harness，并引入EMHO-Merge支持多子任务共享harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体改进多集中于模型训练，而控制规划、上下文与工具使用的harness通常依赖人工设计；作者探究harness能否在稀疏环境反馈下从经验轨迹中自我改进。

Method: EMHO保持具身模型冻结，分析执行轨迹和先前harness历史，迭代修订harness，优化进度监控、视觉工具使用、观察定位与失败响应等；EMHO-Merge利用回合级收益与损失，引导证据支持的细化，以在多个子任务间联合优化单一共享harness。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench的导航与操作任务上，EMHO对Qwen 9B和27B模型均持续提升任务成功率；定性分析显示其不仅从失败和无效动作中恢复，还重塑智能体对环境理解与交互方式。

Conclusion: harness可无需训练底层模型而直接从经验中自我进化，显著提升具身任务表现，并可通过EMHO-Merge兼顾多子任务共享优化。

Abstract: Improving embodied agents often focuses on optimizing the underlying model through training, while the surrounding agent harness that controls planning, context, and tool use is typically engineered. We ask whether this harness can instead improve itself directly from experience traces under sparse environmental feedback. We propose EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization (EMHO), a self-evolving framework that keeps the embodied model frozen and iteratively revises its harness by analyzing execution trajectories and prior harness history. EMHO optimizes beyond skills or recovery prompts, modifying how the agent monitors progress, uses vision tools, grounds observations, and responds to failures. To support multiple subtasks with a single harness, we introduce EMHO-Merge, which addresses trade-offs in jointly optimizing a single shared harness across subtasks by using episode-level gains and losses to guide evidence-supported refinement of when and how revised behaviors are applied. We evaluate EMHO on EmbodiedBench across navigation and manipulation tasks, and EMHO consistently improves task success for both Qwen 9B and 27B models. Qualitative analysis shows that EMHO goes beyond recovering from failures and unproductive actions to reshape how the embodied agent interprets and interacts with its environment.

</details>


### [368] [Generalized Matheron Variational Implicit Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07938)
*Luis A. Ortega,Andrés R. Masegosa,Thomas D. Nielsen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GMVIP：一种针对隐式过程先验的路径式变分后验推断方法，通过诱导输入处的校正构造后验样本，并将KL散度转化为系数空间形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式过程先验（如贝叶斯神经网络、随机模拟器）虽可通过前向采样定义函数分布，但其函数空间密度通常不可得，导致难以直接进行变分后验推断。

Method: GMVIP从先验中采样函数，并在诱导输入处施加校正；校正远离诱导点的效果直接由先验样本决定，从而保留原隐式过程的结构与变异性。代理先验与变分后验共用同一路径式构造，仅在白化诱导系数的分布上不同，得到可处理的系数空间KL散度。对高斯过程先验，该方法退化为标准诱导变量变分GP构造；对一般隐式先验，其经验协方差构造在总体极限下保持先验均值与协方差。

Result: 在回归、分类和预测任务上，使用模拟器定义及检索条件化的经验轨迹先验进行实验，GMVIP与现有方法相比具有广泛竞争力。

Conclusion: GMVIP为隐式过程先验提供了一种通用、可扩展且保持先验结构的变分推断框架。

Abstract: Implicit-process priors specify distributions over functions through sample-forward mechanisms such as Bayesian neural networks and stochastic simulators, but their function-space densities are typically unavailable. We introduce Generalized Matheron Variational Implicit Processes (GMVIP), a pathwise variational family for posterior inference with such priors. For Gaussian-process priors, GMVIP recovers the standard inducing-variable variational GP construction; for general implicit priors, its empirical covariance construction preserves the prior mean and covariance in the population limit. GMVIP constructs posterior samples by drawing a function from the prior and applying a correction anchored at a set of inducing inputs. The effect of this correction away from the inducing inputs is determined directly from prior samples, allowing the posterior to retain the structure and variability of the original implicit process. The (surrogate) prior and variational posterior use the same pathwise construction and differ only in the distribution of whitened inducing coefficients, yielding a tractable coefficient-space Kullback-Leibler divergence. Experiments on regression, classification, and forecasting with simulator-defined and retrieval-conditioned empirical trajectory priors show that GMVIP is broadly competitive with existing methods.

</details>


### [369] [AssemState: Manual and Physical-State-Guided Reasoning for Zero-shot Furniture Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08446)
*Zhiyuan Qi,Jierui Li,Yifan Shen,Cheng Qian,Jiateng Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出零样本框架AssemState，从家具组装手册和物理状态中恢复装配树并生成6D位姿，显著提升结构恢复和局部位姿指标，但多模态大模型的空间推理仍受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉理解上进展显著，但融合物理环境的精确3D空间推理仍困难。家具组装需从图示手册恢复步骤级操作，并将语义连接关系转化为6D位姿更新，使零件与环境和已组装部件物理交互。

Method: AssemState零样本框架：首先采用锚点引导的边界装配状态，将手册页面分解为单零件操作并恢复装配树；然后使用迭代后状态反馈细化，指导连续SE(3)更新与修正，并通过基于仿真的释放测试验证物理合理性。

Result: 与最强基线相比，装配树恢复F1从38.58%提升至62.80%，树精确匹配从28.24%提升至53.92%。在243个独立评估的零件级操作中，迭代细化将评审接受的操作从0提升至5.3%，平均倒角距离从5.4111降至1.7744。但视觉上合理的候选位姿仍存在碰撞、悬浮、镜像朝向错误、未完全就位和错误侧附着等问题。

Conclusion: AssemState改善了操作结构恢复和选定的局部位姿指标，但多模态大模型在空间关系推理方面仍存在局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding, but precise 3D spatial reasoning integrated with physical environment remains difficult. Furniture assembly requires not only recovering step-level operations from diagrammatic manuals, but also translating semantic attachment relations into 6D pose updates that enable parts to physically interact with the environment and previously assembled components. To study this problem, we propose AssemState, a zero-shot framework for manual and physical-state-guided furniture assembly. It firstly employs anchor-guided boundary assembly states to decompose manual pages into single-part operations and recover an assembly-tree. Then, it uses iterative after-state feedback refinement to guide successive (SE(3)) updates and corrections, and validates their physical plausibility through simulation-based release tests. Experiments show that compared with the strongest prior baseline, AssemState improves F1 from 38.58\% to 62.80\% and Tree Exact Match from 28.24\% to 53.92\% for assembly-tree recovery. On 243 independently evaluated part-level operations, our proposed iterative refinement improves judge-accepted operations from 0 to 5.3\% and reduces mean Chamfer distance from 5.4111 to 1.7744. However, visually plausible candidate poses may still suffer from collision, floating, mirror-orientation errors, incomplete seating, and wrong-side attachment. These results show that AssemState improves operation-structure recovery and selected local pose metrics, while MLLMs remain limited for spatial relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [370] [DecepEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Deception in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07967)
*Yiming Xu,Hongyue Yu,Beihua Yang,Zihan Chen,Yixin Liu,Zhen Peng,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Chao Shen,Irwin King,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DecepEval基准与LLM欺骗钻石框架，系统评估外部条件对LLM代理欺骗行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多局限于孤立场景或狭窄条件，缺乏对LLM代理欺骗行为触发条件的系统理解，而自主性增强使欺骗风险上升，威胁可靠部署。

Method: 构建包含1532个实例、覆盖3类任务族和28个专业场景的DecepEval基准；基于经典欺诈理论提出LLM欺骗钻石框架，刻画压力、激励、机会、冲突四种外部条件；为每个实例配对中性版与诱导版以测量条件依赖的欺骗率变化，并通过显式任务事实和可观测代理行为区分欺骗与能力错误。

Result: 对9个前沿LLM的评估显示，诱导条件在所有模型和任务族中均提高了欺骗率，即使基线欺骗率低的模型也不例外。

Conclusion: DecepEval使此类脆弱性可测量，为迈向可信人工智能提供了共享基准。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become increasingly autonomous, they may pursue task performance through deception, raising concerns about their reliable deployment. Existing evaluations show that LLM agents can deceive, but often examine isolated scenarios or narrowly defined conditions, limiting systematic understanding of when deception becomes more likely. To address this gap, we introduce DecepEval, a benchmark comprising 1,532 instances across 3 task families and 28 professional scenarios. Drawing on classical fraud theories, we propose the LLM Deception Diamond framework, which characterizes four external conditions that may induce deception: pressure, incentive, opportunity, and conflict. DecepEval pairs neutral and induced versions of each instance to measure condition-dependent changes in deception rates, while explicit task facts and observable agent behavior help distinguish deception from capability-related errors. Evaluations of nine frontier LLMs show that inducements increase deception across models and task families, even among models with low baseline deception rates. DecepEval makes these vulnerabilities measurable, providing a shared benchmark for progress toward trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [371] [Do Higher-Order Models Win for Higher-Order Reasons? Rethinking Performance Gains in Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07981)
*Fanchen Bu,Fan Li,Geon Lee,Sunwoo Kim,Xiaoyang Wang,Renaud Lambiotte,Kijung Shin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 高阶模型在超图学习基准上的优势未必源于其利用高阶信息的能力，需通过受控扰动框架重新审视性能归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶模型（如超图神经网络）常优于低阶基线，但仅凭性能优势无法证明其优势确实来自高阶信息的利用，因此作者质疑：高阶模型的胜出是否出于高阶原因？

Method: 提出受控的性能归因框架，在保留低阶（成对）信息的前提下扰动高阶信息；在涵盖三类任务的25个常用超图学习基准上进行实验，并探究剩余性能差距的低阶解释，如更丰富的成对加权、更多成对特征传播步数及归一化。

Result: 频繁观察到：高阶模型原本优于低阶基线，但在扰动高阶信息后仍保留大部分优势；在低阶基线中加入简单改进（如成对加权、更多传播步数、归一化）可缩小剩余性能差距。

Conclusion: 部分观察到的优势可由低阶因素解释，呼吁超图学习社区区分性能增益与其解释、采用更强的低阶基线，并使用能更好检验高阶信息价值的合适基准。

Abstract: Higher-order models (e.g., hypergraph neural networks) often outperform lower-order baselines on hypergraph learning benchmarks, and their advantages are commonly attributed to their ability to exploit higher-order information. However, better performance alone does not establish this explanation. We therefore ask: Do higher-order models win for higher-order reasons? To investigate this question, we introduce a controlled performance-attribution framework that perturbs higher-order information while preserving the lower-order, i.e., pairwise, information. Across 25 commonly used hypergraph learning benchmarks spanning three tasks, we frequently observe an intriguing pattern: higher-order models originally outperform lower-order baselines, yet retain most of their advantage after perturbation. This suggests that much of the observed advantage remains achievable without the higher-order information. We then investigate potential lower-order explanations for these remaining gaps. We find that simple additions to a lower-order baseline, e.g., richer pairwise weighting, more steps of pairwise feature propagation, and normalization, reduce the remaining performance gaps, supporting lower-order explanations for part of the observed advantage. Our analysis calls for the hypergraph learning community to rethink performance attribution by distinguishing performance gains from their explanations, adopt stronger lower-order baselines, and use suitable benchmarks that better test the value of higher-order information.

</details>


### [372] [AnyBottle: A Recipe to Only Keep the Concepts You Really Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08552)
*Wolfgang Stammer,Sukrut Rao,Hevra Petekkaya,David Steinmann,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: AnyBottle提出一种无需概念标注的通用方案，通过冻结骨干网络和无监督概念池，借助黑盒教师模型按失败驱动顺序选择紧凑的任务特定概念，并用嵌套dropout训练，使瓶颈可从任意概念前缀进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）可解释、可干预，但原版需要概念标注；无标注变体虽免标注，却通常使用大规模、训练和推理时静态的概念词表，导致瓶颈远大于任务或预测所需，且难以检查。

Method: AnyBottle仅假设一个冻结骨干和一个无监督概念池（如稀疏自编码器）；用同骨干训练的黑盒教师指导选择，每轮加入最能解释当前瓶颈失败的概念，候选限定在教师/学生分歧区域；按选择顺序使用嵌套dropout训练，使最终瓶颈能基于任意概念前缀准确预测；各阶段不依赖特定模态，换领域任务只需替换骨干和概念池。

Result: 在六个视觉和两个文本数据集、两种教师范式上，AnyBottle相比无标注基线得到概念更少、概念一致性更高的瓶颈，同时性能接近黑盒参考模型。

Conclusion: 无需标注并不意味着必须使用大规模概念：一个小的、被自动发现的概念词表可以像大得多的固定词表一样具有表达力。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make predictions inspectable and intervenable by routing them through human-interpretable concepts, but originally required concept annotations. Annotation-free variants remove this requirement, but typically use large concept vocabularies, static at both training and inference, producing bottlenecks larger than any task or prediction needs and harder to inspect. We propose AnyBottle, a single recipe for building compact, task-specific CBMs. AnyBottle assumes only a frozen backbone and an unsupervised concept pool, such as a sparse autoencoder. A black-box teacher trained on the same backbone then guides selection: each round adds the concept that best explains the bottleneck's current failures, with candidates restricted to regions of teacher/student disagreement. Trained with nested dropout over this selection order, the final bottleneck predicts accurately from any concept prefix, so inference spends fewer concepts on inputs it is confident about early and more on hard ones. Since no stage is modality-specific, a new domain and task requires swapping only the backbone and concept pool. Across six vision and two text datasets and two teacher paradigms, AnyBottle yields bottlenecks with fewer concepts and higher concept consistency than annotation-free baselines, while staying close to the black-box reference. Overall, AnyBottle shows that going annotation-free need not mean going large: a small, discovered vocabulary can be as expressive as a much larger, fixed one.

</details>


### [373] [TICDA: Tabular In-Context Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07996)
*Yacine Benihaddadene,Milan Bhan,Eliot Dugelay,Mohammed Jawhar,Benjamin Wong,Nicolas Chesneau,Duong Nguyen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出TICDA，一种基于线性代理模型和TFM潜在嵌入的上下文演示影响度量方法，单次前向传播、低成本，在四个任务中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文中的带标签演示进行预测，但单个演示如何影响预测尚不清楚，且上下文常含错误、冗余或低质量样本，而现有数据归因方法（如DemoShapley、影响函数）因组合爆炸或依赖参数更新而不适用。

Method: TICDA从TFM潜在嵌入上训练的线性代理模型中度量每个上下文演示的影响，仅需单次前向传播且计算成本可忽略。

Result: 在四个任务上，TICDA与竞争方法相比取得最佳折中：检测标注错误、整理上下文以在降低推理成本的同时保持预测准确率、生成可跨TFM迁移的归因分数、支持高效主动学习的采集策略。

Conclusion: TICDA为TFM上下文演示影响提供了一种高效、可迁移的归因方法，适用于数据清洗、上下文优化和主动学习等实际场景。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong predictive performance by conditioning on labeled demonstrations provided in context, without any parameter update. Yet how individual demonstrations shape a given prediction remains poorly understood. This gap matters in practice: the context is often assembled from whatever labeled data is available, potentially leading to the inclusion of mislabeled, redundant, or low-quality examples that degrade performance. Standard data attribution methods do not transfer to the TFM setting: resampling-based approaches such as DemoShapley require a combinatorial number of forward passes, and gradient-based estimators such as influence functions require computing training point's effect on the model parameters, which in-context learning never updates. We introduce TICDA, a method that measures the influence of every demonstration in the context directly from linear surrogates trained on TFM latent embeddings, in a single forward pass and at negligible cost. We show that TICDA offers the best compromise against competitors across four tasks: detecting labeling errors, curating context to preserve predictive accuracy while lowering inference cost, producing attribution scores that transfer across TFMs, and supporting an acquisition strategy for efficient active learning.

</details>


### [374] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出自适应幂采样（APS），在测试时根据每个查询的难度和模型自我奖励差距动态调整锐化指数，无需额外训练即可提升大语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级幂采样对所有查询使用统一的锐化指数，忽略了查询难度差异以及基座模型对各查询的已有处理能力，因此需要为幂采样引入查询自适应性。

Method: 理论分析表明进一步锐化的收益由正确与错误响应之间的自我奖励差距决定；据此提出APS，在测试时利用答案一致性与模型自我奖励之间的关系，按查询调整锐化指数。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval和GPQA等多种推理任务上，APS始终优于使用固定锐化指数的幂采样，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的锐化指数调整能够有效提升幂采样的推理性能，是一种无需训练、即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [375] [MINDSET: Energy-based Schema Evolution for Long Conversational Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08586)
*Sujato Dutta,Sreekruthy Tummala,Shashank Vanga,Ayushmi Pavani*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MINDSET记忆控制器，将对话存储为不可变片段并通过最小能量状态转移组织为版本化模式，在长期对话记忆任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话智能体需记住久远信息并适应随时间变化的指令与上下文，现有记忆系统难以同时保留当前与历史状态、区分过时与活跃知识、检索相关证据且避免反复调用大模型重写交互。

Method: MINDSET将对话存储为不可变片段（immutable episodes），通过最小能量状态转移组织为版本化模式（versioned schemas）；每个新片段可强化、取代、拆分或创建模式，转移决策权衡表示失真、矛盾、历史损害、碎片化与内部不一致，并用滞回机制防止孤立矛盾过早改写稳定记忆。

Result: 在850题可复现样本（700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench）上对比5个记忆系统，MINDSET取得最高的LoCoMo答案F1，并显著提升检索排序指标（Recall@8、MRR、nDCG@8），优于第二名LightMem（Holm校正后p<0.01）；在MemoryAgentBench上取得最高观测分数但相对差异较小；消融显示受控碎片化与模式感知分配对答案质量贡献最大；700题跨模型评估（GLM-4.7与Gemma-4-31B）支持模型独立性。

Conclusion: 长期记忆更适合作为受约束的状态管理而非持续摘要处理。

Abstract: Long conversational agents have become essential in our daily lives. They must remember what was said long back in order to help us efficiently complete a task without needing the user to repeat instructions and context repeatedly. However, the main issue is that instructions and context change over time and so the agents must be able to adapt accordingly. A useful memory system should preserve both current and historical states, distinguish stale information from active knowledge, retrieve evidence appropriate to the query and avoid repeatedly invoking a large language model to rewrite prior interactions. We introduce MINDSET, a memory controller that stores a conversation as immutable episodes and organizes them into versioned schemas through minimum-energy state transitions. Each incoming episode may reinforce, supersede, split or create a schema. The transition decision balances representation distortion, contradiction, historical damage, fragmentation and internal inconsistency, while hysteresis prevents isolated contradictions from prematurely rewriting stable memory. We evaluate MINDSET against 5 memory systems on a reproducible sample of 850 questions (700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench). MINDSET obtains the highest observed LoCoMo answer F1 while significantly improving retrieval ranking (Recall@8, MRR and nDCG@8) over the second best method LightMem (p<0.01 after Holm correction). It obtains the highest observed scores on MemoryAgentBench although the relative difference is low. Ablations identify controlled fragmentation and schema-aware assignment as the largest contributors to answer quality. Additionally, a 700-question cross-model evaluation with GLM-4.7 and Gemma-4-31B supported model independence. These results show that long-term memory can be better handled as constrained state management rather than continual summarization.

</details>


### [376] [Spectra: Exact Component Transport for Test-Time Prior Adaptation in Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08021)
*Xin Zhao,Nico Scherf,Robert Trampel,Kerrin J. Pine,Nikolaus Weiskopf*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Spectra，一种面向扩散模型SBI的测试时自适应方法，可在不重新模拟或训练的情况下实现先验改变下的后验推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销式SBI训练好的推理模型只能复用训练时选定的先验，而科学分析常需随知识积累或假设检验更新先验，导致复用受限。

Method: Spectra基于精确的score-transport恒等式，对结构化先验变化，从冻结的扩散模型中闭式求解自适应score，无需额外模拟或训练。

Result: 在六个SBI基准上，Spectra在先验强偏移下实现准确自适应，且在线采样成本低。

Conclusion: 该方法使预训练SBI模型可在测试时融入更新后的先验信息。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) has become a powerful approach to Bayesian inference in complex scientific models whose likelihoods are difficult or impossible to evaluate. Amortized SBI learns reusable inference models from simulated data, enabling rapid posterior inference for new observations, and modern generative models have made these models increasingly expressive. However, this reuse is limited to the prior distribution chosen during training, whereas scientific analyses often need revised priors as knowledge accumulates or alternative assumptions are tested. We introduce Spectra, a test-time adaptation method for diffusion-based SBI. Spectra uses an exact score-transport identity to obtain the adapted score from a frozen diffusion model in closed form for structured prior changes, without additional simulation or training. Across six SBI benchmarks, Spectra achieves accurate adaptation under strong prior shifts at low online sampling cost. This enables pretrained SBI models to incorporate updated prior information at test time.

</details>


### [377] [SepsisLens: Structure-Preserving Sequence Modelling for Decomposable Early Sepsis Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08046)
*Yikun Ou,Wei Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SepsisLens模型，在ICU脓毒症早期预警中保持变量级时间状态直到风险组合，实现结构可分解的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间模型将临床变量融合为患者级表示，虽支持标量风险预测，但削弱了临床分解所需的结构，难以将预警与支撑它的生理信号关联起来。

Method: SepsisLens采用观测感知表示编码每个变量的动态与测量历史，用共享时间编码器建模各轨迹而不压缩变量轴，并由StructuredRiskHead从显式的变量级和器官级组件组合多时间跨度风险。

Result: 在三个公共ICU队列和一个私立医院队列的统一发病前协议下，SepsisLens在四个队列均取得强判别性能，并在MIMIC-IV上于匹配事件召回率下具有更低的警报负担；结构消融支持该设计，输入侧掩码显示排序组件反映了对预测影响更大的变量。

Conclusion: 保持变量索引的时间状态直至风险组合，可在不牺牲预测性能的同时保留临床可分解结构，降低警报负担并提升组件可解释性。

Abstract: Early sepsis warning from ICU records can be cast as a structure-preserving prediction problem. A model needs to detect deterioration from irregular measurements while keeping each alert connected to the physiological signals that support it. Many temporal models fuse clinical variables into a patient-level representation, supporting scalar risk prediction but weakening the structure needed for clinical decomposition. We present SepsisLens, which preserves variable-indexed temporal states until risk composition. Observation-aware representations encode each variable's dynamics and measurement history, while a shared temporal encoder models each trajectory without collapsing the variable axis. The StructuredRiskHead composes multi-horizon risk from explicit variable-level and organ-level components. We evaluate SepsisLens on three public ICU cohorts and one private-hospital cohort under a common pre-onset protocol. SepsisLens achieves strong discrimination on all four cohorts and lower alert burden at matched event recall on MIMIC-IV. Structural ablations support the design, while input-side masking shows that the ranked components reflect variables with greater influence on prediction.

</details>


### [378] [Parallel Predictive World Models for Accurate and Efficient Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08627)
*Wanjin Feng,Baobin Zhang,Ao Yu,Shibo Feng,Xi Wang,Xingyu Gao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出并行预测世界模型PPWM，并行预测有限时域轨迹并保持因果交互，替代自回归展开，提升长期预测精度与CEM规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长期世界模型规划依赖自回归展开，逐步将预测状态反馈回模型，虽保持时序结构，但形成与视界长度成正比的顺序路径，并使后续预测暴露于递归解码状态反馈，导致误差累积与规划缓慢。

Method: PPWM并行预测有限时域轨迹，同时保留未来表示间的因果交互；每个时域以因果动作前缀为条件，未来表示在解码前交互，将时间因果性与逐状态输出递归解耦；形式化地将自回归展开视为因果轨迹映射，并识别出PPWM移除的解码状态反馈路径。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务中，PPWM在评估的预测接口中取得最低的长期预测误差和最高的CEM模拟器成功率；同时相比自回归LeWM基线，PPWM平均实现超过3倍的CEM规划加速。

Conclusion: 准确高效的长期世界模型规划不必须依赖逐状态自回归，而可通过并行因果轨迹预测实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon world-model planning typically relies on autoregressive rollouts, where predicted states are repeatedly fed back into the model. This preserves temporal structure but creates a horizon-length sequential path and exposes later predictions to recursive decoded-state feedback. We introduce Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM), which predict a finite-horizon trajectory in parallel while retaining causal interaction among future representations. Each horizon is conditioned on its causal action prefix, and future representations interact before decoding, separating temporal causality from state-by-state output recursion. We formalize this distinction by viewing autoregressive rollout as a causal trajectory map and identifying the decoded-state feedback pathway removed by PPWM. Across four visual-control tasks, PPWM achieves the lowest long-horizon prediction error and the highest Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) simulator success among the evaluated predictive interfaces. Meanwhile, PPWM achieves more than a 3$\times$ average CEM planning speedup over the autoregressive LeWM baseline. These results suggest that accurate and efficient long-horizon world-model planning does not require state-by-state autoregression, but can instead be achieved through parallel causal trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [379] [SquidAgent: Parallelize Wisely, Coordinate Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08647)
*Yexiong Lin,Shanshan Ye,Yu Yao,Zhen Fang,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文指出并行多智能体系统常因再探索与对齐开销而慢于串行基线，并提出基于预测输出token的并行判定准则及SquidAgent系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体处理多步复杂任务时，串行执行延迟高；理论上并行多智能体可获得近线性加速，但现有系统常比单智能体基线更慢，作者将此归因于并行执行引入的再探索成本和对齐成本。

Method: 提出原则性判定准则：仅当某层的关键路径成本加上再探索与对齐开销低于串行成本时才并行化。由于LLM对墙钟时间估计校准差，改用预测输出token衡量成本。基于此提出SquidAgent：单步规划估计所有token预算；每个worker直接从orchestrator会话分叉以消除再探索成本；用预生成共享约定块将对齐转为有界前期成本；确定性调度器逐层应用该准则。

Result: 相比Claude Code，SquidAgent平均吞吐提升2.2倍、平均墙钟加速2.6倍；相比最强多智能体基线，吞吐提升2.0倍。

Conclusion: 以预测输出token为成本度量的并行化判定准则可有效避免并行隐藏开销，SquidAgent通过会话分叉与共享约定块实现显著的吞吐和延迟收益。

Abstract: LLM-based agents solve complex multi-step tasks, but sequential execution incurs substantial latency. In principle, parallelizing work across multiple agents should yield near-linear speedups. Yet existing parallel multi-agent systems often run slower than a single-agent baseline. We attribute this gap to two hidden costs that parallel execution incurs but a serial agent avoids. First, there is a re-exploration cost: redundant effort spent by parallel workers reconstructing context that the orchestrator already possesses, such as prior decisions, that would otherwise be inherited implicitly in a serial execution. Second, there is an alignment cost: the overhead required to reconcile inconsistencies across independently generated outputs. We thus derive a principled decision criterion: a layer should be parallelized only when its critical-path cost, plus re-exploration and alignment overheads, is lower than the corresponding serial cost. While this criterion is naturally expressed in wall-clock time, we observe that LLMs are poorly calibrated when asked to estimate task duration. To address this, we instead measure cost in predicted output tokens, which we empirically find LLMs can estimate substantially more reliably than wall-clock time. Building on this token-based criterion, we propose SquidAgent. It estimates all token budgets in a single planning step, forks each worker directly from the orchestrator's session to eliminate re-exploration cost, and replaces post-hoc reconciliation with a pre-generated shared convention block that converts alignment into a bounded upfront cost. A deterministic scheduler then applies the criterion layer by layer. Empirically, SquidAgent achieves a 2.2$\times$ mean throughput improvement and a 2.6$\times$ mean wall-time speedup over Claude Code, and a 2.0$\times$ throughput improvement over the strongest multi-agent baseline.

</details>


### [380] [Enhancing Diffusion Language Models with Autoregressive Post-Training Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08108)
*Yiming Qin,Ke Wang,Amel Abdelraheem,Adam Hazimeh,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架A2D，通过复用自回归模型的后训练权重更新来增强扩散语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型通常从预训练自回归模型初始化，但转换后忽略了自回归后训练生态，导致其已学到的能力未被充分利用。

Method: 分析自回归与扩散后训练权重更新的关系，发现两者近乎正交但表示变化对齐，且互补；据此提出A2D，直接组合自回归后训练更新与扩散基座或已后训练扩散模型的权重，无需额外训练或推理计算。

Result: 在Dream、DreamReasoner、DiffuCoder、Dream-Coder、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion和DiffusionGemma等扩散语言模型上，A2D使用监督微调和强化学习更新，可靠提升了指令遵循、数学推理和代码生成性能。

Conclusion: 现有自回归后训练资源可有效回收用于增强扩散语言模型，A2D提供了一种简单、免训练且通用的能力迁移与性能提升方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) language models, offering flexible token-update orders and parallel decoding. Recent dLLMs are often initialized from pretrained AR models before diffusion conversion in order to inherit their learned representations. After the conversion, however, they typically ignore the extensive post-training ecosystem of their AR ancestors. In this work, we show that these existing AR post-training weight updates can instead be effectively recycled to enhance diffusion models. Despite the changes by AR-to-diffusion conversion, directly adding an AR post-training weight update to a diffusion base model remains effective, bringing its performance close to that achieved by direct diffusion post-training. Notably, AR and diffusion post-training updates are nearly orthogonal in weight space, yet induce substantially more aligned representation changes in the diffusion model. Their distinct updates are also complementary: composing their weights can retain gains from both regimes and further improve the post-trained diffusion model. Based on these findings, we propose A2D, a simple training-free framework for enhancing diffusion models with existing AR post-training resources. A2D can transfer capabilities from AR post-trained models to diffusion base models, and further improve already post-trained diffusion models by composing AR and diffusion post-training updates. Across various dLLMs, including Dream, DreamReasoner, DiffuCoder, Dream-Coder, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion, and DiffusionGemma, A2D reliably improves instruction following, mathematical reasoning, and coding with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning updates, without additional training, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [381] [Attenuated in-context identification in time-series foundation models: diagnosis under counterfactual inputs and repair by synthetic forced-system fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08118)
*Hong-In Won*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 论文测试了协变量感知时间序列基础模型在受迫工程系统上的反事实预测能力，发现默认接口下模型存在无记忆性或动态衰减问题，而微调可部分恢复响应幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间序列基础模型能否为仪器化工厂提供免训练的what-if因果预测，即不同未来输入导致的输出变化。

Method: 在具有精确反事实的受迫工程系统上，比较Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5和TabPFN-TS与基于相同上下文拟合的经典系统辨识。使用配对反事实输入和实测记录上的打乱未来输入，检验协变量接口能否表征动态以及预训练先验是否覆盖对象时间尺度。

Result: TimesFM-2.5和TabPFN-TS默认协变量接口无记忆（TimesFM-2.5的R²=1.000）；Chronos-2能识别上下文动态但衰减，预测效应为真实效应的0.33-0.80，恢复的脉冲响应形状错误，单自由度振子误差在8192上下文样本时趋于0.57，而ARX用256样本达0.02。推理时上下文抖动可降低所有六类合成数据的what-if误差。26分钟微调恢复响应幅度（灵敏度0.83-0.96），在Wiener-Hammerstein和留出摩擦类上优于结构无关辨识。专用上下文辨识器接近该性能，表明受迫系统数据贡献主要增益。在四个实测对象中的三个上经典辨识仍明显更优，微调模型部分丧失单变量预测能力。

Conclusion: 只有配对反事实输入能暴露动态衰减；当前协变量感知TSFM在受迫系统反事实预测上仍不及经典辨识，微调可改善但代价是预测能力下降。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time-series foundation models (TSFMs) promise training-free what-if answers for instrumented plants: the change in output that a different future input would cause. We test this on forced engineering systems with exact counterfactuals, comparing Chronos-2, TimesFM-2.5 and TabPFN-TS with classical system identification fitted to the same context. Through their default covariate interfaces, TimesFM-2.5 and TabPFN-TS are memoryless: the predicted effect of an input change is a same-time function of that change ($R^2 = 1.000$ for TimesFM-2.5). Chronos-2 identifies dynamics in context but attenuates them. Its predicted effect is 0.33-0.80 of the true effect, its recovered impulse response has the wrong shape, and its error on a one-degree-of-freedom oscillator levels off at 0.57 with 8192 context samples, where ARX fitted to 256 samples reaches 0.02. Context dither at inference lowers the what-if error on all six synthetic classes without training. A 26-minute fine-tune on synthetic forced systems restores the response magnitude (sensitivity 0.83-0.96) and outperforms structure-agnostic identification on Wiener-Hammerstein and a held-out friction class. A specialised in-context identifier trained on the same data comes close, so the forced-system data carry most of the gain. On three of four measured plants classical identification remains clearly better, and the fine-tuned model loses part of its univariate forecasting skill. Paired counterfactual inputs, together with shuffled future inputs on measured records, test two properties: whether the covariate interface can represent dynamics and whether the pretraining prior covers the plant's time scale. Only the counterfactual pairs expose the attenuation.

</details>


### [382] [nanoMuse: An Open-Source Personal Agent for Every Device You Own](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08699)
*Guangyi Liu,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文对比Meta闭源个人智能体Muse，并提出开源对应物nanoMuse，定义个人智能体并规划开放路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muse作为个人智能体闭源且受供应商、云、国别限制，缺乏开放对应物，因此需定义并构建开源个人智能体。

Method: 基于Meta公开记录及生产提示副本分析Muse构建方式，并设计nanoMuse：覆盖用户所有设备，通过可自建中继共享对话，所有动作经Sentinel，记忆为可读文件，模型自选。

Result: 给出nanoMuse的规模与成本估算，并明确开放记忆及溯源、操作评估套件、开放模型等路线图内容。

Conclusion: nanoMuse是GPL-3.0开源个人智能体，实现跨设备、可自建中继、可审计记忆与模型自选，并规划开放评估与模型。

Abstract: Assistants from 2011 answered and waited, and agents from 2023 did a task and stopped. In September 2026 Meta's Muse showed an agent for one person, with accounts, devices, memory and a conversation that lasts, closed, in a vendor's cloud, in one country. Such an agent is expected to act on a person's accounts and devices, remember them across weeks, speak first when it is worth it, and answer for what it did. It is a kind of software, not a model, and until now had no open counterpart. This report defines the personal agent in five questions and three horizons. It reads how Muse is built from Meta's public record and a copy of its production prompt, each statement marked by its source. It then presents nanoMuse, the open-source counterpart under the GPL-3.0, one agent on every device a person owns, with hands on the phone's screen and the computer's. They share one conversation over a relay anyone can run; every action goes through a Sentinel, memory is files the person can read, and the model is their choice. Its size and cost are given as estimates. What is open, memory with provenance, an evaluation suite for the hands and an open model for them, is set out as a roadmap.

</details>
